# 项目四讲解文档：功能与完整代码版

本文档是 `docs/讲解文档.md` 的完整代码版本，原文档保持不变。

本文档不只列出代码位置：每个功能章节后面都嵌入该功能对应的完整 Python 源码，直到函数、类、测试和启动脚本都包含在文档中。项目当前所有 Python 文件都分配到唯一章节，避免重复贴同一份源码。

- 源码总行数：**9469** 行
- 基线提交：`4941bae`

## 二、先把完整消息链路串起来

```text
客户端界面
  -> ClientSession
  -> Connection.send
  -> encode_frame：4 字节长度头 + UTF-8 JSON
  -> TCP
  -> server.read_frame
  -> Session.run / Session._dispatch
  -> @register 注册的 handler
  -> Hub / Room / Table
  -> Session.send：进入该连接的有界发送队列
  -> Session._writer_loop
  -> 客户端 Connection._pump
  -> ClientSession._handle
  -> 界面事件
```

这条链路是讲解的主线。下面每一节先说明功能，再给出该功能对应的全部代码。

## 三、P0-1：TCP 分帧、粘包与半包

**对应要求**

- TCP 消息必须有长度或等价边界字段。
- 正确处理粘包、半包、非法长度、非法 JSON 和非法 UTF-8。
- 不能假设一次 `recv` 对应一条消息。

**功能与代码的对应关系**

`encode_frame()` 负责发送端编码，`read_frame()` 负责接收端还原消息，
`FrameError` 负责把非法帧转成协议错误。`tests/test_framing.py` 覆盖半包、
粘包、长度为 0、长度超限、非法 JSON 和非对象载荷。

**现场讲解结论**

服务端先精确读取 4 字节长度，再精确读取该长度的消息体；因此 TCP 的字节流
不会把两条消息粘在一起，也不会在消息只到一半时提前解析。长度超限时没有
读取消息体，字节流已经错位，所以必须断开。

### 本功能完整代码

### `server/framing.py`（57 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""分帧层：4 字节大端长度前缀 + UTF-8 JSON。

处理要求：

* 粘包 / 半包 —— 一律用 `readexactly` 读足长度再解析，不假设"一次 recv 一条消息"；
* 非法长度 —— 长度为 0、超过上限、读不满，都抛 `FrameError`；
* 非法内容 —— 非 UTF-8、非 JSON、非对象，同样抛 `FrameError`。

调用方（会话层）负责把 `FrameError` 转成 error 帧并决定是否断开。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import struct

HEADER = struct.Struct(">I")


class FrameError(Exception):
    """帧不合法。`code` 直接作为 error 帧的错误码。"""

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, fatal: bool = False) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"{code}: {message}")
        self.code = code
        self.message = message
        # fatal=True 表示字节流已经错位（例如长度超限时没有读取消息体），只能断开
        self.fatal = fatal


def encode_frame(payload: dict) -> bytes:
    """把消息字典编码成带长度前缀的一帧。"""
    body = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    return HEADER.pack(len(body)) + body


async def read_frame(reader: asyncio.StreamReader, max_bytes: int) -> dict:
    """从流里读出一帧。"""
    header = await reader.readexactly(HEADER.size)
    (length,) = HEADER.unpack(header)
    if length == 0:
        raise FrameError("BAD_FRAME", "帧长度为 0")
    if length > max_bytes:
        raise FrameError("BAD_FRAME", f"帧长度 {length} 超过上限 {max_bytes}", fatal=True)

    body = await reader.readexactly(length)
    try:
        payload = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8"))
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        raise FrameError("BAD_FRAME", "帧不是合法的 UTF-8") from None
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        raise FrameError("BAD_FRAME", "帧不是合法的 JSON") from None

    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise FrameError("BAD_FRAME", "消息必须是 JSON 对象")
    return payload
````

### `tests/test_framing.py`（87 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""分帧层单元测试：粘包、半包、非法长度与非法内容。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import struct

import pytest

from server.framing import HEADER, FrameError, encode_frame, read_frame

MAX = 65536


def reader_with(data: bytes) -> asyncio.StreamReader:
    reader = asyncio.StreamReader()
    reader.feed_data(data)
    reader.feed_eof()
    return reader


async def test_round_trip():
    payload = {"type": "ping", "data": {"echo": "中文"}, "request_id": "r1"}
    frame = encode_frame(payload)
    body = json.loads(frame[4:].decode("utf-8"))
    assert body == payload
    assert struct.unpack(">I", frame[:4])[0] == len(frame) - 4
    assert await read_frame(reader_with(frame), MAX) == payload


async def test_half_packet_is_buffered():
    """半包：一次只到一半的字节，读帧必须等剩下的。"""
    frame = encode_frame({"type": "ping", "data": {}})
    reader = asyncio.StreamReader()
    reader.feed_data(frame[:10])
    task = asyncio.create_task(read_frame(reader, MAX))
    await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
    assert not task.done(), "不应在消息不完整时返回"
    reader.feed_data(frame[10:])
    assert await task == {"type": "ping", "data": {}}


async def test_merged_packets_are_split():
    """粘包：两条消息挤在一次投递里，必须分两次读出。"""
    blob = encode_frame({"type": "ping", "data": {"n": 1}}) + encode_frame(
        {"type": "ping", "data": {"n": 2}}
    )
    reader = reader_with(blob)
    first = await read_frame(reader, MAX)
    second = await read_frame(reader, MAX)
    assert first["data"]["n"] == 1
    assert second["data"]["n"] == 2


async def test_zero_length_frame():
    with pytest.raises(FrameError) as exc:
        await read_frame(reader_with(HEADER.pack(0)), MAX)
    assert exc.value.code == "BAD_FRAME"
    assert exc.value.fatal is False


async def test_oversize_frame_is_fatal():
    with pytest.raises(FrameError) as exc:
        await read_frame(reader_with(HEADER.pack(MAX + 1)), MAX)
    assert exc.value.fatal is True, "长度超限时消息体没读，字节流已错位"


async def test_invalid_json():
    body = "not json".encode()
    with pytest.raises(FrameError) as exc:
        await read_frame(reader_with(HEADER.pack(len(body)) + body), MAX)
    assert exc.value.code == "BAD_FRAME"
    assert exc.value.fatal is False


async def test_invalid_utf8():
    body = b"\xff\xfe\xfd"
    with pytest.raises(FrameError):
        await read_frame(reader_with(HEADER.pack(len(body)) + body), MAX)


async def test_non_object_payload():
    body = b"[1,2,3]"
    with pytest.raises(FrameError) as exc:
        await read_frame(reader_with(HEADER.pack(len(body)) + body), MAX)
    assert "JSON 对象" in exc.value.message
````

## 四、P0-2：服务端入口、会话循环、发送队列、背压与心跳

**对应要求**

- 服务端同时处理多个客户端。
- 每个连接有独立发送队列。
- 慢客户端不得拖住其他客户端。
- 实现心跳、静默超时和断线清理。
- 非法消息、未知类型和处理器异常不能拖垮整个服务端。

**功能与代码的对应关系**

- `server/main.py`：启动 `asyncio.start_server`，创建心跳扫描任务。
- `server/session.py`：每条连接一个读循环、一个写协程和有界发送队列。
- `Session.send()`：条数与字节数双阈值。
- `Session._writer_loop()`：`drain()` 只阻塞当前连接的写协程。
- `Session._on_drop()` / `_mark_slow_consumer()`：慢客户端判定和断开。
- `heartbeat_sweeper()`：静默连接发 `ping`，超时后清理。
- `config.py`：这些机制的参数集中配置。

**现场讲解结论**

广播只是把消息放入各成员的独立队列，不需要等待所有客户端真正写完网络。
一个只连不读的客户端只会堵住自己的写协程；达到阈值后，服务端清空该连接
积压、返回 `SLOW_CONSUMER` 并断开。其他连接继续工作。

### 本功能完整代码

### `server/main.py`（98 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""服务端入口：`python -m server`。

启动后会打印监听地址与房间列表，方便本机多开客户端时确认连哪里。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import itertools
import logging
import socket
import sys

if __package__ in (None, ""):  # 允许 `python server/main.py` 直接运行
    sys.path.insert(0, str(__import__("pathlib").Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from config import settings  # noqa: E402
from server import handlers, protocol  # noqa: E402,F401  导入即注册处理器
from server.hub import Hub  # noqa: E402
from server.session import Session  # noqa: E402

logger = logging.getLogger("server")

_conn_counter = itertools.count(1)


async def heartbeat_sweeper(hub: Hub) -> None:
    """心跳与清理：静默就发 ping，彻底静默就清理，顺带回收过期的重连预约。"""
    cfg = hub.cfg
    while True:
        await asyncio.sleep(cfg.sweep_interval_ms / 1000)
        now = protocol.now_ms()
        for session in list(hub.sessions.values()):
            if session.closed:
                continue
            idle = now - session.last_seen_ms
            if idle >= cfg.ping_interval_ms and now - session.last_ping_ms >= cfg.ping_interval_ms:
                session.ping()
            if idle >= cfg.idle_timeout_ms:
                logger.info("[%s] 静默 %d ms，判定失效", session.conn_id, idle)
                await session.close_for_idle()
        hub.sweep_reservations()
        for room in hub.rooms.values():
            if room.table is not None:
                await room.table.tick(now)


async def handle_connection(reader: asyncio.StreamReader, writer: asyncio.StreamWriter,
                            hub: Hub) -> None:
    cfg = hub.cfg  # 用 Hub 的配置，便于测试起不同参数的实例
    conn_id = f"c{next(_conn_counter):04d}"
    if hub.pending_count() >= cfg.max_pending_connections:
        logger.warning("未登录连接过多，拒绝 %s", conn_id)
        writer.close()
        return
    session = Session(conn_id, reader, writer, hub, cfg)
    await session.run()


def local_addresses() -> list[str]:
    """列出本机可用 IPv4 地址，跨机测试时告诉对方该填哪个。"""
    addresses: list[str] = []
    try:
        hostname = socket.gethostname()
        for info in socket.getaddrinfo(hostname, None, socket.AF_INET):
            addr = info[4][0]
            if addr not in addresses:
                addresses.append(addr)
    except OSError:  # pragma: no cover
        pass
    return addresses


async def serve() -> None:
    hub = Hub(settings)
    server = await asyncio.start_server(
        lambda r, w: handle_connection(r, w, hub), settings.host, settings.port
    )
    sweeper = asyncio.create_task(heartbeat_sweeper(hub))
    print(f"服务端已启动: {settings.host}:{settings.port}", flush=True)
    print(f"本机可用地址: {', '.join(local_addresses()) or '未检测到'}", flush=True)
    print(f"房间: {[room.name for room in hub.rooms.values()]}", flush=True)
    print(f"分帧: 4 字节长度前缀，单帧上限 {settings.max_frame_bytes} 字节", flush=True)
    async with server:
        await server.serve_forever()
    sweeper.cancel()


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
    try:
        asyncio.run(serve())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n服务端已停止")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
````

### `server/session.py`（300 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""会话：一条 TCP 连接的读写、发送队列与背压、握手保护、心跳与重连凭据。

要点：

* 每个连接一个**有界**发送队列（条数 + 字节数双阈值）和一个写协程；
* 消息分三类：可丢（房间广播）/ 必达（私信、系统、结算）/ 控制（心跳，不进队列直接写）；
* 队列持续积压到上限即判定慢客户端：清空积压、回一条 error、随后断开；
* 读循环只有一条退出路径（`finally` 统一清理），任何异常都不重试；
* 时间统一用服务端单调时钟。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
from collections import deque

from . import protocol
from .framing import FrameError, encode_frame, read_frame
from .protocol import ErrCode, error, make

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Session:
    def __init__(self, conn_id: str, reader: asyncio.StreamReader,
                 writer: asyncio.StreamWriter, hub, cfg) -> None:
        """连接级状态：发送队列、最后活跃时间、丢弃计数与重连 token。"""
        self.conn_id = conn_id
        self.reader = reader
        self.writer = writer
        self.hub = hub
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.nickname: str | None = None
        self.token: str | None = None
        self.room: str | None = None

        self.queue: asyncio.Queue[bytes] = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=cfg.send_queue_max)
        self._queue_bytes = 0
        self.last_seen_ms = protocol.now_ms()
        self.last_ping_ms = 0
        self.bad_frames = 0
        self.unknown_types = 0
        self.dropped = 0
        self.queue_high_water = 0
        self.drop_times: deque[int] = deque()
        self.slow_consumer = False
        self.close_reason = ""
        self.closed = False
        self._writer_task: asyncio.Task | None = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 生命周期 ----

    async def run(self) -> None:
        """会话主循环：握手→逐帧分发；任何异常都只有 finally 一个出口。"""
        self._writer_task = asyncio.create_task(self._writer_loop())
        self.hub.add_session(self)
        try:
            first = await self._handshake()
            if first is None:
                return
            await self._dispatch(first)
            while not self.closed:
                try:
                    payload = await read_frame(self.reader, self.cfg.max_frame_bytes)
                except FrameError as exc:
                    # 只有确认字节流仍然对齐（消息体已读满）才可以继续；否则必须断开
                    if not self._reject_bad_frame(exc):
                        break
                    continue
                self.last_seen_ms = protocol.now_ms()
                await self._dispatch(payload)
        except FrameError as exc:
            self._reject_bad_frame(exc)
        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
            self.send(error(ErrCode.HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT, "未在限时内完成登录"))
            self.close_reason = "handshake timeout"
            logger.info("[%s] 握手超时", self.conn_id)
        except asyncio.IncompleteReadError:
            pass  # 对端正常或异常关闭
        except (ConnectionError, OSError) as exc:
            logger.info("[%s] 连接中断: %s", self.conn_id, exc)
        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - 兜底，绝不让异常逃逸出去
            logger.exception("[%s] 会话异常", self.conn_id)
        finally:
            await self.close(self.close_reason or "session end")

    async def _handshake(self) -> dict | None:
        """首个消息必须在限时内到达，且是 login 或 resume。"""
        timeout = self.cfg.handshake_timeout_ms / 1000
        payload = await asyncio.wait_for(
            read_frame(self.reader, self.cfg.max_frame_bytes), timeout
        )
        self.last_seen_ms = protocol.now_ms()
        if payload.get("type") not in ("login", "resume", "register"):
            self.send(error(ErrCode.NOT_LOGGED_IN, "首个消息必须是 login / resume / register",
                            payload.get("request_id")))
            await self.close("handshake rejected")
            return None
        return payload

    async def _dispatch(self, payload: dict) -> None:
        """按 type 查注册表；未知类型回错误，连续多次则断开。"""
        msg_type = payload.get("type")
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        if not isinstance(msg_type, str):
            self.bad_frames += 1
            self.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, "缺少 type 字段", request_id))
            return

        handler = protocol.get_handler(msg_type)
        if handler is None:
            self.unknown_types += 1
            self.send(
                error(ErrCode.UNKNOWN_TYPE, f"未知消息类型 {msg_type}", request_id,
                      supported=protocol.SUPPORTED_TYPES)
            )
            if self.unknown_types >= self.cfg.bad_frame_limit:
                await self.close("too many unknown types")
            return

        data = payload.get("data") or {}
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            data = {}
        try:
            await handler(self, data, request_id)
        except Exception:
            # 单个处理器的 bug 不该拖垮整条连接
            logger.exception("[%s] 处理 %s 时异常", self.conn_id, msg_type)
            self.send(error(ErrCode.INTERNAL, f"处理 {msg_type} 时内部错误", request_id))

    def _reject_bad_frame(self, exc: FrameError) -> bool:
        """记录非法帧并回错误；返回是否还能继续读（字节流是否仍然对齐）。"""
        self.bad_frames += 1
        self.send(error(exc.code, exc.message))
        logger.warning("[%s] 非法帧(%d/%d): %s", self.conn_id, self.bad_frames,
                       self.cfg.bad_frame_limit, exc)
        if exc.fatal:
            return False
        if self.bad_frames >= self.cfg.bad_frame_limit:
            self.send(error(ErrCode.TOO_MANY_BAD_FRAMES, "非法帧过多"))
            return False
        return True

    async def _writer_loop(self) -> None:
        """唯一的写协程：从有界队列取字节写入 socket，背压在这里体现。"""
        try:
            while True:
                data = await self.queue.get()
                self._queue_bytes = max(0, self._queue_bytes - len(data))
                try:
                    # drain() 只阻塞本连接的写协程：慢客户端会卡住自己的队列，
                    # 不会拖住其他连接（这正是 M1 慢客户端对照实验验证的点）。
                    self.writer.write(data)
                    await self.writer.drain()
                finally:
                    self.queue.task_done()
        except (asyncio.CancelledError, ConnectionError, OSError):
            pass

    async def close(self, reason: str = "", keep_reservation: bool = True) -> None:
        """统一的退出路径：摘除状态、发完残余消息、关连接、取消写协程。可重复调用。"""
        if self.closed:
            return
        self.closed = True
        self.close_reason = reason
        nickname = self.nickname  # logout 会清空，先留一份用于日志
        self.hub.logout(self, keep_reservation=keep_reservation)
        if self._writer_task is not None and not self._writer_task.done():
            try:
                await asyncio.wait_for(self.queue.join(), timeout=0.5)
            except (asyncio.TimeoutError, asyncio.CancelledError):
                pass
            self._writer_task.cancel()
        try:
            self.writer.close()
        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
            pass
        logger.info(
            "[%s] 关闭(%s) 昵称=%s 丢弃=%d 非法帧=%d 队列峰值=%d",
            self.conn_id, reason or "-", nickname, self.dropped,
            self.bad_frames, self.queue_high_water,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 发送 ----

    def send(self, payload: dict, droppable: bool = False) -> bool:
        """入队；队列满（条数或字节数）时按类型处理。"""
        if self.closed or self.slow_consumer:
            return False
        data = encode_frame(payload)
        if self.queue.full() or self._queue_bytes + len(data) > self.cfg.send_queue_max_bytes:
            self._on_drop(droppable)
            return False
        self._queue_bytes += len(data)
        self.queue.put_nowait(data)
        self.queue_high_water = max(self.queue_high_water, self.queue.qsize())
        return True

    def send_control(self, payload: dict) -> bool:
        """控制类消息（心跳）：不进业务队列，直接写传输层，避免被积压挡住。"""
        if self.closed:
            return False
        try:
            self.writer.write(encode_frame(payload))
            return True
        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
            return False

    def ping(self) -> bool:
        """发心跳：控制帧直接写传输层，绕过业务队列，避免被积压拖慢。"""
        self.last_ping_ms = protocol.now_ms()
        return self.send_control(make("ping", {"interval_ms": self.cfg.ping_interval_ms}))

    def _on_drop(self, droppable: bool) -> None:
        """队列满时的丢弃处理：按时间窗口统计，达到阈值判定慢客户端。"""
        self.dropped += 1
        self.hub.dropped_total += 1
        now = protocol.now_ms()
        self.drop_times.append(now)
        while self.drop_times and now - self.drop_times[0] > self.cfg.slow_consumer_window_ms:
            self.drop_times.popleft()
        if not droppable:
            logger.warning("[%s] 队列已满，丢弃必达消息（累计 %d）", self.conn_id, self.dropped)
        if len(self.drop_times) >= self.cfg.slow_consumer_drop_limit:
            self._mark_slow_consumer()

    def _mark_slow_consumer(self) -> None:
        """判定慢客户端：清空积压、回一条 error、随后断开。"""
        if self.slow_consumer or self.closed:
            return
        self.slow_consumer = True
        self.hub.slow_consumer_disconnects += 1
        logger.warning(
            "[%s] 判定慢客户端：窗口内丢弃 %d 条，断开连接",
            self.conn_id, len(self.drop_times),
        )
        while not self.queue.empty():
            try:
                self.queue.get_nowait()
                self.queue.task_done()
            except asyncio.QueueEmpty:  # pragma: no cover
                break
        self._queue_bytes = 0
        try:
            self.queue.put_nowait(
                encode_frame(error(ErrCode.SLOW_CONSUMER, "发送队列持续积压，连接将被断开"))
            )
        except asyncio.QueueFull:  # pragma: no cover
            pass
        asyncio.get_running_loop().create_task(self._close_later(0.3))

    async def _close_later(self, delay: float) -> None:
        """延迟关闭：先让错误帧发出去，再断开连接。"""
        await asyncio.sleep(delay)
        await self.close("slow consumer", keep_reservation=False)

    async def close_for_idle(self) -> None:
        """心跳判活失败：静默超时，清理连接（不保留重连预约）。"""
        self.hub.idle_timeouts += 1
        self.send_control(error(ErrCode.SESSION_EXPIRED, "长时间无响应，连接已断开"))
        await self.close("idle timeout", keep_reservation=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 视图 ----

    def room_snapshot(self) -> dict:
        room = self.hub.room_of(self)
        return room.snapshot() if room else {}

    def welcome_payload(self, request_id: str | None = None) -> dict:
        return make(
            "welcome",
            {
                "conn_id": self.conn_id,
                "nickname": self.nickname,
                "account": self.nickname,
                "token": self.token,
                "room": self.room,
                "rooms": self.hub.room_list(),
                "online": self.hub.online_count(),
                "reconnect_grace_ms": self.cfg.reconnect_grace_ms,
            },
            request_id,
        )

    def resumed_payload(self, request_id: str | None = None) -> dict:
        room = self.hub.room_of(self)
        return make(
            "resumed",
            {
                "conn_id": self.conn_id,
                "nickname": self.nickname,
                "account": self.nickname,
                "token": self.token,
                "room": self.room,
                "users": room.nicknames() if room else [],
                "online": self.hub.online_count(),
            },
            request_id,
        )
````

### `config.py`（97 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""集中配置（运行期零第三方依赖，直接读环境变量与默认值）。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field


def _env_int(name: str, default: int) -> int:
    raw = os.environ.get(name)
    return default if raw is None or raw == "" else int(raw)


def _env_str(name: str, default: str) -> str:
    raw = os.environ.get(name)
    return default if raw is None or raw == "" else raw


def _env_accounts(name: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    """解析 `DDZ_EXTRA_ACCOUNTS="bot0:pw,bot1:pw"` 这样的额外账号表。"""
    raw = os.environ.get(name, "")
    accounts: dict[str, str] = {}
    for item in raw.split(","):
        item = item.strip()
        if not item:
            continue
        account, _, password = item.partition(":")
        if account.strip():
            accounts[account.strip()] = password.strip() or "123456"
    return accounts


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    # 服务端监听：本机实验用 127.0.0.1，跨机时改为 0.0.0.0 并放行防火墙
    host: str = _env_str("DDZ_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
    port: int = _env_int("DDZ_PORT", 9011)

    # 分帧：4 字节大端长度 + UTF-8 JSON
    max_frame_bytes: int = _env_int("DDZ_MAX_FRAME_BYTES", 65536)
    max_text_chars: int = _env_int("DDZ_MAX_TEXT_CHARS", 500)

    # 握手保护：未登录连接的存活时间与非法帧上限
    handshake_timeout_ms: int = _env_int("DDZ_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS", 10_000)
    bad_frame_limit: int = _env_int("DDZ_BAD_FRAME_LIMIT", 3)
    max_pending_connections: int = _env_int("DDZ_MAX_PENDING_CONNECTIONS", 50)

    # 每连接发送队列（M0 先用固定上限，M1 补丢弃/断开策略与统计）
    send_queue_max: int = _env_int("DDZ_SEND_QUEUE_MAX", 128)
    send_queue_max_bytes: int = _env_int("DDZ_SEND_QUEUE_MAX_BYTES", 65536)
    slow_consumer_drop_limit: int = _env_int("DDZ_SLOW_CONSUMER_DROP_LIMIT", 50)
    slow_consumer_window_ms: int = _env_int("DDZ_SLOW_CONSUMER_WINDOW_MS", 10_000)

    # 心跳与判活：收到任意合法帧都刷新 last_seen；静默超过 idle 即清理
    ping_interval_ms: int = _env_int("DDZ_PING_INTERVAL_MS", 10_000)
    idle_timeout_ms: int = _env_int("DDZ_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS", 30_000)
    sweep_interval_ms: int = _env_int("DDZ_SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS", 5_000)

    # 断线重连：宽限期内昵称保留，凭 token 恢复
    reconnect_grace_ms: int = _env_int("DDZ_RECONNECT_GRACE_MS", 60_000)

    # 昵称限制
    max_nickname_chars: int = _env_int("DDZ_MAX_NICKNAME_CHARS", 32)

    # 账号体系（课程演示）：预置 5 个账号，密码统一 123456。
    # 默认**严格模式**：只有预置账号与额外账号能登录（拼错账号会被拒绝并给出提示）。
    # 需要更多身份（压测 10 客户端等）时用 DDZ_EXTRA_ACCOUNTS="bot0:pw,bot1:pw" 批量注入；
    # 也可以临时打开 DDZ_AUTO_REGISTER=1，让未预置账号首次登录即注册。
    extra_accounts: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=lambda: _env_accounts("DDZ_EXTRA_ACCOUNTS"))
    account_passwords: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=lambda: {
        "wanjia1": "123456",
        "wanjia2": "123456",
        "wanjia3": "123456",
        "wanjia4": "123456",
        "wanjia5": "123456",
    })
    auto_register: bool = _env_int("DDZ_AUTO_REGISTER", 0) == 1
    # 私信历史（仅内存，服务端重启即清空）：每个会话最多保留多少条
    max_private_history: int = _env_int("DDZ_MAX_PRIVATE_HISTORY", 300)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        # 额外账号并入账号表（不覆盖预置账号的密码）
        for account, password in self.extra_accounts.items():
            self.account_passwords.setdefault(account, password)

    # 默认房间：大厅是登录后自动进入的房间，不可退出
    lobby_room: str = _env_str("DDZ_LOBBY_ROOM", "大厅")
    chat_rooms: tuple[str, ...] = ("聊天房",)
    # 牌桌：三人一桌，其余人可作观战
    table_rooms: tuple[str, ...] = ("牌桌1", "牌桌2", "牌桌3")
    seats_per_table: int = _env_int("DDZ_SEATS_PER_TABLE", 3)
    max_spectators: int = _env_int("DDZ_MAX_SPECTATORS", 2)
    # 断线托管：轮到自己后多久自动出牌（毫秒）
    auto_play_delay_ms: int = _env_int("DDZ_AUTO_PLAY_DELAY_MS", 3000)


settings = Settings()
````

### `tests/test_m1.py`（248 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""M1：发送队列背压、慢客户端隔离、心跳判活与 token 重连。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import dataclasses
import socket
import struct
import time

import pytest

from client.net import Connection, ServerError, encode_frame
from config import settings
from server.hub import Hub
from server.protocol import ErrCode, make
from server.session import Session


class FakeWriter:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.chunks: list[bytes] = []

    def write(self, data: bytes) -> None:
        self.chunks.append(data)

    async def drain(self) -> None:
        await asyncio.sleep(0)

    def close(self) -> None:
        return None


def make_session(cfg, hub=None) -> Session:
    hub = hub or Hub(cfg)
    session = Session("c0001", None, FakeWriter(), hub, cfg)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    session.nickname = "queue-user"
    return session


async def wait_until(predicate, timeout: float = 3.0, interval: float = 0.05) -> bool:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if predicate():
            return True
        await asyncio.sleep(interval)
    return False


# --------------------------------------------------------- 队列与背压 ----


async def test_queue_drops_broadcasts_when_full():
    cfg = dataclasses.replace(settings, send_queue_max=4, slow_consumer_drop_limit=100)
    session = make_session(cfg)

    accepted = sum(1 for _ in range(4) if session.send(make("room_msg", {"text": "x"}),
                                                     droppable=True))
    assert accepted == 4, "前 4 条应入队"
    assert session.send(make("room_msg", {"text": "y"}), droppable=True) is False
    assert session.dropped == 1
    assert session.hub.dropped_total == 1
    assert session.slow_consumer is False, "偶尔丢一条不该判定慢客户端"
    await session.close("test", keep_reservation=False)


async def test_queue_byte_limit_also_drops():
    cfg = dataclasses.replace(settings, send_queue_max=1000, send_queue_max_bytes=200,
                              slow_consumer_drop_limit=100)
    session = make_session(cfg)
    text = "x" * 100
    for _ in range(10):
        session.send(make("room_msg", {"text": text}), droppable=True)
    assert session.dropped > 0, "字节数超限也要能挡住"
    await session.close("test", keep_reservation=False)


async def test_slow_consumer_is_marked_and_disconnected():
    cfg = dataclasses.replace(settings, send_queue_max=2, slow_consumer_drop_limit=3,
                              slow_consumer_window_ms=60_000)
    session = make_session(cfg)
    for _ in range(10):
        session.send(make("room_msg", {"text": "flood"}), droppable=True)
    assert session.slow_consumer is True
    assert session.hub.slow_consumer_disconnects == 1
    assert session.queue.qsize() <= 1, "判定后应清空积压，只留一条提示"
    assert await wait_until(lambda: session.closed, timeout=2.0), "判定后应断开连接"


async def test_must_deliver_message_dropped_when_full():
    cfg = dataclasses.replace(settings, send_queue_max=1, slow_consumer_drop_limit=100)
    session = make_session(cfg)
    session.send(make("room_msg", {"text": "a"}), droppable=True)
    assert session.send(make("private_msg", {"text": "b"}), droppable=False) is False
    assert session.dropped == 1
    await session.close("test", keep_reservation=False)


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 心跳 ----


async def test_heartbeat_ping_and_idle_cleanup(server_factory):
    server = await server_factory(ping_interval_ms=200, idle_timeout_ms=700,
                                  sweep_interval_ms=100, reconnect_grace_ms=200)
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port, auto_pong=False)
    await conn.login("sleepy")

    ping = await conn.wait_for("ping", timeout=2.0)
    assert "interval_ms" in ping["data"]

    assert await wait_until(lambda: server.hub.idle_timeouts == 1, timeout=3.0), \
        "不回应心跳应被判活失败"
    assert await wait_until(lambda: server.online == 0, timeout=2.0)
    assert server.hub.reserved_count() == 1, "超时后应保留重连预约"

    # 宽限期过后预约被回收
    assert await wait_until(lambda: server.hub.reserved_count() == 0, timeout=3.0)
    await conn.close()


async def test_auto_pong_keeps_connection_alive(server_factory):
    server = await server_factory(ping_interval_ms=150, idle_timeout_ms=500,
                                  sweep_interval_ms=80)
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)  # auto_pong=True
    await conn.login("awake")
    await asyncio.sleep(1.2)
    assert server.hub.idle_timeouts == 0, "自动回应心跳的客户端不该被清理"
    assert server.online == 1
    await conn.close()


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 重连 ----


async def test_resume_restores_nickname_and_room(server):
    first = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    welcome = await first.login("rejoin")
    token = welcome["data"]["token"]
    await first.join_room("聊天房")
    await first.close()

    assert await wait_until(lambda: server.hub.reserved_count() == 1, timeout=2.0)

    second = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    resumed = await second.resume(token)
    data = resumed["data"]
    assert data["nickname"] == "rejoin"
    assert data["room"] == "聊天房", "重连应回到原房间"
    assert data["token"] != token, "重连后应换发新 token"
    assert server.online == 1

    # 旧 token 不能再用
    third = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await third.resume(token)
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.SESSION_EXPIRED
    await second.close()
    await third.close()


async def test_nickname_reserved_during_grace(server):
    first = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await first.login("holder")
    await first.close()
    assert await wait_until(lambda: server.hub.reserved_count() == 1, timeout=2.0)

    other = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await other.login("holder")
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.ACCOUNT_LOGGED_IN
    assert "重连" in exc.value.message
    await other.close()


async def test_resume_after_grace_expires(server_factory):
    server = await server_factory(reconnect_grace_ms=150, sweep_interval_ms=50)
    first = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    token = (await first.login("late"))["data"]["token"]
    await first.close()

    assert await wait_until(lambda: server.hub.reserved_count() == 1, timeout=2.0)
    assert await wait_until(lambda: server.hub.reserved_count() == 0, timeout=3.0)
    late = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await late.resume(token)
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.SESSION_EXPIRED

    # 预约过期后昵称应被释放
    other = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    welcome = await other.login("late")
    assert welcome["data"]["nickname"] == "late"
    await late.close()
    await other.close()


# ------------------------------------------- 慢客户端不影响其他人 ----


def raw_slow_client(host: str, port: int, nickname: str, room: str) -> socket.socket:
    """连上、登录、进房间，然后**永不读取**（制造真实背压）。"""
    sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_RCVBUF, 2048)
    sock.connect((host, port))
    sock.sendall(encode_frame({"type": "login", "data": {"nickname": nickname},
                               "request_id": "r1"}))
    time.sleep(0.2)
    sock.sendall(encode_frame({"type": "join_room", "data": {"room": room},
                               "request_id": "r2"}))
    return sock


async def test_slow_client_does_not_block_others(server_factory):
    """对照实验：慢客户端把服务端发送队列堵满时，正常客户端仍要快。"""
    server = await server_factory(send_queue_max=4, slow_consumer_drop_limit=5,
                                  slow_consumer_window_ms=60_000)
    room = "聊天房"

    slow = raw_slow_client(server.host, server.port, "slowpoke", room)
    await asyncio.sleep(0.3)

    fast = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await fast.login("speedy")
    await fast.join_room(room)

    payload = "z" * 480
    latencies: list[float] = []
    for index in range(200):
        start = time.perf_counter()
        await fast.send("room_msg", {"text": f"{payload}-{index}"})
        latencies.append((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000)
        await asyncio.sleep(0.002)

    # 正常客户端自己的消息应当正常收到（说明广播链路没有被慢客户端堵死）
    got = await fast.wait_for("room_msg", timeout=3.0,
                              match={"from": "speedy", "text": f"{payload}-199"})
    assert got["data"]["from"] == "speedy"
    assert max(latencies) < 200, f"发送不应被阻塞: max={max(latencies):.1f} ms"

    # 慢客户端应当被丢弃计数，最终被判定并断开
    assert await wait_until(
        lambda: server.hub.dropped_total > 0 or server.hub.slow_consumer_disconnects > 0,
        timeout=5.0,
    ), "慢客户端应触发丢弃或断开"
    assert await wait_until(lambda: server.hub.slow_consumer_disconnects >= 1, timeout=8.0), \
        "持续积压应最终判定为慢客户端"

    slow.close()
    await fast.close()
````

## 五、P0-3：协议注册表、账号、房间、私信、群聊与重连

**对应要求**

- 客户端可以登录、退出、查看房间、加入/离开房间、房间聊天和私信。
- 房间不存在、消息非法、账号重复登录等情况要返回明确原因。
- 服务端维护用户和房间状态。
- 房间广播不能串到其他房间，私信只能到目标用户。
- 服务端支持 token 重连，重复账号不能同时在线。

**功能与代码的对应关系**

- `server/protocol.py`：消息信封、消息类型表、错误码、处理器注册表。
- `server/hub.py`：全局房间表、在线账号表、私信历史、重连预约。
- `Room.broadcast()`：只遍历本房间成员。
- `Hub.join()`：切换房间并广播两边成员变化。
- `Hub.login()`：校验账号密码与唯一在线限制。
- `Hub.resume()`：凭 token 恢复昵称和房间，并换发新 token。
- `Hub.create_group()` / `delete_group()`：群聊创建和解散。
- `server/handlers.py`：每个协议消息的服务端入口。

**现场讲解结论**

客户端提交的是协议请求，服务端统一查账号、查房间、查目标在线状态并写共享
状态。`Room.broadcast()` 和 `find_by_nickname()` 决定投递范围，因此房间隔离
和私信定向是服务端保证的，不依赖客户端自觉。

### 本功能完整代码

### `server/protocol.py`（156 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""消息类型、错误码与处理器注册表。

消息信封：

    客户端 → 服务端  {"type": "login", "request_id": "...", "data": {...}}
    服务端 → 客户端  {"type": "welcome", "request_id": "...", "data": {...}, "ts_ms": 123}

处理器用装饰器注册，新增一种消息类型只需加一个函数（现场修改演示用）。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from typing import Awaitable, Callable

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 消息类型 ----

# 客户端 → 服务端
C2S_TYPES = (
    "login",
    "register",
    "resume",
    "pong",
    "logout",
    "ping",
    "list_rooms",
    "list_users",
    "list_conversations",
    "get_history",
    "join_room",
    "leave_room",
    "create_group",
    "delete_group",
    "room_ready",
    "bid",
    "play",
    "pass",
    "spectate",
    "game_state",
    "auto_play",
    "room_msg",
    "private_msg",
)

# 服务端 → 客户端推送
S2C_TYPES = (
    "welcome",
    "resumed",
    "error",
    "ping",
    "pong",
    "room_list",
    "user_list",
    "conversations",
    "history",
    "joined",
    "left",
    "room_users",
    "room_msg_ack",
    "user_joined",
    "user_left",
    "user_reconnecting",
    "group_created",
    "group_deleted",
    "logged_out",
    "room_msg",
    "private_msg",
    "game_start",
    "game_state",
    "bid",
    "play",
    "pass",
    "game_over",
    "table_state",
    "spectating",
)

SUPPORTED_TYPES = tuple(dict.fromkeys(C2S_TYPES))  # 未知类型提示里列出的可发送类型


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 错误码 ----

class ErrCode(str):
    BAD_FRAME = "BAD_FRAME"
    UNKNOWN_TYPE = "UNKNOWN_TYPE"
    NOT_LOGGED_IN = "NOT_LOGGED_IN"
    NICKNAME_TAKEN = "NICKNAME_TAKEN"
    INVALID_NICKNAME = "INVALID_NICKNAME"
    ROOM_NOT_FOUND = "ROOM_NOT_FOUND"
    ALREADY_IN_ROOM = "ALREADY_IN_ROOM"
    NOT_IN_ROOM = "NOT_IN_ROOM"
    USER_NOT_FOUND = "USER_NOT_FOUND"
    MESSAGE_TOO_LONG = "MESSAGE_TOO_LONG"
    HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT = "HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT"
    BAD_MESSAGE = "BAD_MESSAGE"
    TOO_MANY_BAD_FRAMES = "TOO_MANY_BAD_FRAMES"
    SLOW_CONSUMER = "SLOW_CONSUMER"
    SESSION_EXPIRED = "SESSION_EXPIRED"
    ALREADY_LOGGED_IN = "ALREADY_LOGGED_IN"
    ROOM_FULL = "ROOM_FULL"
    NOT_SEATED = "NOT_SEATED"
    MATCH_NOT_STARTED = "MATCH_NOT_STARTED"
    INVALID_BID = "INVALID_BID"
    INTERNAL = "INTERNAL"
    UNKNOWN_ACCOUNT = "UNKNOWN_ACCOUNT"
    BAD_CREDENTIALS = "BAD_CREDENTIALS"
    ACCOUNT_LOGGED_IN = "ACCOUNT_LOGGED_IN"
    ACCOUNT_EXISTS = "ACCOUNT_EXISTS"
    WEAK_PASSWORD = "WEAK_PASSWORD"
    GROUP_NOT_FOUND = "GROUP_NOT_FOUND"
    NOT_GROUP_OWNER = "NOT_GROUP_OWNER"
    GROUP_MEMBER_OFFLINE = "GROUP_MEMBER_OFFLINE"


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 构造函数 ----

def now_ms() -> int:
    return int(time.monotonic() * 1000)


def make(msg_type: str, data: dict | None = None, request_id: str | None = None) -> dict:
    payload: dict = {"type": msg_type, "data": data or {}}
    if request_id:
        payload["request_id"] = request_id
    payload["ts_ms"] = now_ms()
    return payload


def error(code: str, message: str, request_id: str | None = None, **extra) -> dict:
    data = {"code": code, "message": message}
    data.update(extra)
    return make("error", data, request_id)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ 注册表 ----

Handler = Callable[..., Awaitable[None]]
_HANDLERS: dict[str, Handler] = {}


def register(msg_type: str) -> Callable[[Handler], Handler]:
    """把处理器登记到注册表；重复注册会覆盖（便于热改）。"""

    def decorator(func: Handler) -> Handler:
        _HANDLERS[msg_type] = func
        return func

    return decorator


def get_handler(msg_type: str) -> Handler | None:
    return _HANDLERS.get(msg_type)


def registered_types() -> list[str]:
    return sorted(_HANDLERS)
````

### `server/hub.py`（468 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""房间、在线状态与重连预约。

约定：

* 大厅是登录后自动进入的房间，不允许退出；
* 房间广播只发给本房间成员，房间之间互相隔离；
* 成员变化会推送房间成员列表，客户端据此显示在线列表；
* 掉线后昵称不会立刻释放：进入 `reconnect_grace_ms` 的"预约"状态，
  凭 token 可以恢复昵称与房间，超时才正式注销并广播离开。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import secrets
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from .protocol import ErrCode, error, make, now_ms
from .table import Table


def _edit_distance(a: str, b: str) -> int:
    """简易编辑距离，只用来判断"是否只差一个字符"。"""
    if a == b:
        return 0
    if abs(len(a) - len(b)) > 1:
        return 2
    if len(a) > len(b):
        a, b = b, a
    if len(a) == len(b):
        return sum(1 for x, y in zip(a, b) if x != y)
    i = j = 0
    skipped = False
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] != b[j]:
            if skipped:
                return 2
            skipped = True
            j += 1
            continue
        i += 1
        j += 1
    return 1

if TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover
    from .session import Session


@dataclass
class Reservation:
    """断线后为昵称与房间保留的预约。"""

    nickname: str
    room: str
    token: str
    expires_at_ms: int
    expired_reason: str = "断开超时"


class Room:
    def __init__(self, name: str, kind: str = "chat") -> None:
        """初始化房间：kind 区分聊天室/牌桌/群聊，members 以 conn_id 为键。"""
        self.name = name
        self.kind = kind
        self.members: dict[str, "Session"] = {}
        self.owner: str | None = None      # 群聊的群主账号
        self.table: Table | None = None

    def add(self, session: "Session") -> None:
        """把在线会话加入成员表（conn_id 作为键，断线时可精确移除）。"""
        self.members[session.conn_id] = session

    def remove(self, session: "Session") -> None:
        """从成员表移除会话；重复调用是安全的。"""
        self.members.pop(session.conn_id, None)

    def nicknames(self) -> list[str]:
        """返回房间内成员的账号列表（用于在线列表推送）。"""
        return sorted(s.nickname for s in self.members.values() if s.nickname)

    def broadcast(self, payload: dict, droppable: bool = False,
                  exclude: "Session | None" = None) -> int:
        """向全体成员投递，返回实际投递人数（丢弃的会话不计入）。"""
        sent = 0
        for member in list(self.members.values()):
            if member is exclude:
                continue
            if member.send(payload, droppable=droppable):
                sent += 1
        return sent

    def snapshot(self) -> dict:
        """房间摘要：名字/类型/人数/群主，供房间列表使用。"""
        return {"name": self.name, "kind": self.kind, "count": len(self.members),
                "owner": self.owner}


class Hub:
    """全局状态：房间表、连接表、昵称表、重连预约表。"""

    def __init__(self, cfg) -> None:
        """全局状态：房间表、会话表、账号表、重连预约与私信历史。"""
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.rooms: dict[str, Room] = {}
        self.sessions: dict[str, "Session"] = {}
        self.by_nickname: dict[str, "Session"] = {}
        self.reservations: dict[str, Reservation] = {}   # token -> 预约
        self.reserved_names: dict[str, str] = {}         # 昵称 -> token
        self.rooms[cfg.lobby_room] = Room(cfg.lobby_room, kind="lobby")
        for name in cfg.chat_rooms:
            self.rooms[name] = Room(name, kind="chat")
        for name in cfg.table_rooms:
            room = Room(name, kind="doudizhu")
            room.table = Table(room, cfg)
            self.rooms[name] = room
        self.dropped_total = 0
        self.slow_consumer_disconnects = 0
        self.idle_timeouts = 0
        self._group_seq = 0
        self.private_log: dict[tuple[str, str], list[dict]] = {}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 连接 ----

    def add_session(self, session: "Session") -> None:
        """登记新连接（未登录也登记，用于统计待登录连接数）。"""
        self.sessions[session.conn_id] = session

    def pending_count(self) -> int:
        """尚未完成登录的连接数（用于限制半开连接）。"""
        return sum(1 for s in self.sessions.values() if not s.nickname)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 登录 ----

    def _new_token(self) -> str:
        """生成 32 字节十六进制重连令牌。"""
        return secrets.token_hex(16)

    def login(self, session: "Session", account: str,
              password: str = "") -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None]:
        """登录：校验账号与密码、签发 token、进入大厅。

        * 预置账号（见 config.account_passwords）密码必须匹配；
        * 未预置的账号：`auto_register` 打开时首次登录即注册（密码随本次输入），
          关闭时报"账号不存在"，即严格模式。
        * 同一账号只能有一个在线连接，重复登录会被拒。
        """
        account = account.strip()
        if not account or len(account) > self.cfg.max_nickname_chars:
            return False, ErrCode.INVALID_NICKNAME, (
                f"账号需为 1~{self.cfg.max_nickname_chars} 个字符"
            )
        if any(ch.isspace() for ch in account):
            return False, ErrCode.INVALID_NICKNAME, "账号不能包含空白字符"

        expected = self.cfg.account_passwords.get(account)
        if expected is None:
            if not self.cfg.auto_register:
                # 拼错一两个字母是最常见的登录失败原因，直接给出候选
                near = self._near_account(account)
                hint = f"，是不是想输入 {near}？" if near else ""
                return False, ErrCode.UNKNOWN_ACCOUNT, f"账号不存在{hint}"
            self.cfg.account_passwords[account] = password   # 首次登录即注册
        elif password != expected:
            return False, ErrCode.BAD_CREDENTIALS, "账号或密码不正确"

        if account in self.by_nickname:
            return False, ErrCode.ACCOUNT_LOGGED_IN, "该账号已在其它客户端登录"
        if account in self.reserved_names:
            return False, ErrCode.ACCOUNT_LOGGED_IN, "该账号刚刚掉线，正在重连保护中（60 秒）"

        session.nickname = account
        session.token = self._new_token()
        self.by_nickname[account] = session
        self._enter_room(session, self.cfg.lobby_room)
        return True, None, None

    def _near_account(self, account: str) -> str | None:
        """在账号表里找一个"像打错了"的候选（大小写不同或差一个字符）。"""
        lowered = account.lower()
        for known in self.cfg.account_passwords:
            if known.lower() == lowered:
                return known
        for known in self.cfg.account_passwords:
            if _edit_distance(known.lower(), lowered) <= 1:
                return known
        return None

    def online_accounts(self) -> list[str]:
        """当前所有在线账号（不区分房间）。"""
        return sorted(self.by_nickname)

    def register(self, account: str, password: str) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None]:
        """创建账号：只校验规则与重名，不做邮箱/验证码之类。"""
        account = account.strip()
        if not account or len(account) > self.cfg.max_nickname_chars:
            return False, ErrCode.INVALID_NICKNAME, (
                f"账号需为 1~{self.cfg.max_nickname_chars} 个字符"
            )
        if any(ch.isspace() for ch in account):
            return False, ErrCode.INVALID_NICKNAME, "账号不能包含空白字符"
        if len(password) < 4:
            return False, ErrCode.WEAK_PASSWORD, "密码至少 4 位"
        lowered = account.lower()
        if any(known.lower() == lowered for known in self.cfg.account_passwords):
            return False, ErrCode.ACCOUNT_EXISTS, "该账号已存在，请直接登录"
        self.cfg.account_passwords[account] = password
        return True, None, None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 私信 ----

    def record_private(self, sender: str, target: str, text: str, ts_ms: int) -> None:
        """记录一条私信（按账号对归档，只保留最近若干条）。"""
        key = tuple(sorted((sender, target)))
        box = self.private_log.setdefault(key, [])
        box.append({"from": sender, "to": target, "text": text, "ts_ms": ts_ms})
        if len(box) > self.cfg.max_private_history:
            del box[: len(box) - self.cfg.max_private_history]

    def private_history(self, account: str, peer: str) -> list[dict]:
        """取两个账号之间的私信历史（按时间正序）。"""
        return list(self.private_log.get(tuple(sorted((account, peer))), []))

    def conversations_of(self, account: str) -> list[dict]:
        """会话摘要列表：和谁聊过、最后一条、条数与时间（按时间倒序）。"""
        items: list[dict] = []
        for (a, b), messages in self.private_log.items():
            if account not in (a, b) or not messages:
                continue
            peer = b if a == account else a
            last = messages[-1]
            items.append({
                "peer": peer,
                "count": len(messages),
                "last_text": last["text"],
                "last_from": last["from"],
                "last_ts_ms": last["ts_ms"],
            })
        items.sort(key=lambda item: item["last_ts_ms"], reverse=True)
        return items

    def resume(self, session: "Session", token: str) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None]:
        """凭 token 恢复会话：昵称、房间一并恢复，并换发新 token。"""
        reservation = self.reservations.get(token)
        if reservation is None:
            return False, ErrCode.SESSION_EXPIRED, "会话不存在或已失效，请重新登录"
        if reservation.expires_at_ms <= now_ms():
            self._expire_reservation(reservation)
            return False, ErrCode.SESSION_EXPIRED, "重连宽限期已过，请重新登录"
        if reservation.nickname in self.by_nickname:
            return False, ErrCode.NICKNAME_TAKEN, "该昵称已被其他连接占用"

        del self.reservations[token]
        self.reserved_names.pop(reservation.nickname, None)

        session.nickname = reservation.nickname
        session.token = self._new_token()
        self.by_nickname[reservation.nickname] = session
        room_name = reservation.room if reservation.room in self.rooms else self.cfg.lobby_room
        room = self._enter_room(session, room_name, resumed=True)
        if room.table is not None:
            room.table.on_resume(session)
        return True, None, None

    def _enter_room(self, session: "Session", room_name: str, resumed: bool = False) -> Room:
        """把会话放进指定房间并广播成员变化（resumed 表示重连恢复）。"""
        room = self.rooms[room_name]
        room.add(session)
        session.room = room.name
        room.broadcast(
            make(
                "user_joined",
                {"nickname": session.nickname, "room": room.name, "resumed": resumed},
            )
        )
        self.broadcast_room_users(room)
        self.broadcast_room_list()
        return room

    def logout(self, session: "Session", keep_reservation: bool = True) -> None:
        """断线清理。

        keep_reservation=True 且已登录：昵称与房间进入宽限期（可凭 token 重连），
        从在线名单与房间成员中移除，并通知房间内其他人"正在重连"。
        """
        nickname = session.nickname
        room = self.rooms.get(session.room) if session.room else None

        if nickname and room is not None:
            room.remove(session)
            if room.table is not None:
                room.table.on_disconnect(session)
        if nickname and self.by_nickname.get(nickname) is session:
            del self.by_nickname[nickname]

        if nickname and keep_reservation and session.token and room is not None:
            reservation = Reservation(
                nickname=nickname,
                room=room.name,
                token=session.token,
                expires_at_ms=now_ms() + self.cfg.reconnect_grace_ms,
            )
            self.reservations[session.token] = reservation
            self.reserved_names[nickname] = session.token
            room.broadcast(
                make("user_reconnecting", {"nickname": nickname, "room": room.name,
                                           "grace_ms": self.cfg.reconnect_grace_ms}),
                droppable=True,
            )
            self.broadcast_room_users(room)
        elif nickname and room is not None:
            room.broadcast(make("user_left", {"nickname": nickname, "room": room.name}))
            self.broadcast_room_users(room)

        self.sessions.pop(session.conn_id, None)
        session.nickname = None
        session.room = None
        self.broadcast_room_list()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 预约 ----

    def _expire_reservation(self, reservation: Reservation) -> None:
        """预约过期：正式注销账号并广播离开。"""
        self.reservations.pop(reservation.token, None)
        self.reserved_names.pop(reservation.nickname, None)
        room = self.rooms.get(reservation.room)
        if room is not None:
            room.broadcast(
                make("user_left", {"nickname": reservation.nickname, "room": room.name})
            )
            self.broadcast_room_users(room)
        self.broadcast_room_list()

    def sweep_reservations(self) -> int:
        """清理过期的重连预约，返回清理数量。"""
        now = now_ms()
        expired = [r for r in self.reservations.values() if r.expires_at_ms <= now]
        for reservation in expired:
            self._expire_reservation(reservation)
        return len(expired)

    def reserved_count(self) -> int:
        """当前处于重连保护期的账号数。"""
        return len(self.reservations)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 房间 ----

    def join(self, session: "Session", room_name: str) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None]:
        """切换房间：离开原房间、加入新房间，并广播两边的成员变化。"""
        room = self.rooms.get(room_name)
        if room is None:
            return False, ErrCode.ROOM_NOT_FOUND, f"房间 {room_name} 不存在"
        if session.room == room_name:
            return False, ErrCode.ALREADY_IN_ROOM, f"你已经在 {room_name}"

        previous = self.rooms.get(session.room) if session.room else None
        if previous is not None:
            previous.remove(session)
            previous.broadcast(
                make("user_left", {"nickname": session.nickname, "room": previous.name})
            )
            self.broadcast_room_users(previous)

        self._enter_room(session, room_name)
        return True, None, None

    def leave(self, session: "Session") -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None]:
        """回到大厅（大厅不可退出）。"""
        if session.room == self.cfg.lobby_room:
            return False, ErrCode.NOT_IN_ROOM, "大厅是默认房间，不能退出"
        return self.join(session, self.cfg.lobby_room)

    def room_of(self, session: "Session") -> Room | None:
        """取会话当前所在房间。"""
        return self.rooms.get(session.room) if session.room else None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 群聊 ----

    def create_group(self, owner: "Session",
                     members: list[str]) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None, str | None]:
        """邀请若干在线账号建群：所有被邀请者会被直接拉进新房间。"""
        picked: list[str] = []
        for raw in members:
            name = raw.strip() if isinstance(raw, str) else ""
            if not name or name == owner.nickname or name in picked:
                continue
            if name not in self.by_nickname:
                return False, ErrCode.GROUP_MEMBER_OFFLINE, f"{name} 当前不在线", None
            picked.append(name)
        if not picked:
            return False, ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, "请至少选择一位在线用户", None

        self._group_seq += 1
        room_name = f"群聊{self._group_seq}"
        room = Room(room_name, kind="group")
        room.owner = owner.nickname
        self.rooms[room_name] = room

        self.join(owner, room_name)
        for name in picked:
            member = self.by_nickname.get(name)
            if member is not None:
                self.join(member, room_name)

        room.broadcast(make("group_created", {
            "room": room_name, "owner": owner.nickname, "members": room.nicknames(),
        }))
        self.broadcast_room_list()
        return True, None, None, room_name

    def delete_group(self, session: "Session", room_name: str) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None]:
        """解散群聊（仅群主）：成员回到大厅，房间被移除。"""
        room = self.rooms.get(room_name)
        if room is None or room.kind != "group":
            return False, ErrCode.GROUP_NOT_FOUND, "群聊不存在"
        if room.owner != session.nickname:
            return False, ErrCode.NOT_GROUP_OWNER, "只有群主可以解散群聊"

        room.broadcast(make("group_deleted", {"room": room_name, "owner": session.nickname}),
                       droppable=True)
        for member in list(room.members.values()):
            self.join(member, self.cfg.lobby_room)
        self.rooms.pop(room_name, None)
        self.broadcast_room_list()
        return True, None, None

    def groups_of(self, session: "Session") -> list[dict]:
        """列出所有群聊摘要（统计/调试用）。"""
        return [room.snapshot() for room in self.rooms.values() if room.kind == "group"]

    def find_by_nickname(self, nickname: str) -> "Session | None":
        """按账号查找在线会话（私信投递用）。"""
        return self.by_nickname.get(nickname)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- 推送 ----

    def room_list(self) -> list[dict]:
        """房间摘要列表，客户端大厅直接展示。"""
        return [room.snapshot() for room in self.rooms.values()]

    def broadcast_room_list(self) -> None:
        """把房间列表推给所有已登录会话。"""
        payload = make("room_list", {"rooms": self.room_list()})
        for session in list(self.sessions.values()):
            if session.nickname:
                session.send(payload)

    def broadcast_room_users(self, room: Room) -> None:
        """把某房间的成员列表推给该房间成员。"""
        room.broadcast(
            make("room_users", {"room": room.name, "users": room.nicknames()}),
            droppable=True,
        )

    def online_count(self) -> int:
        """在线账号数。"""
        return len(self.by_nickname)

    async def close_all(self, reason: str = "server shutdown") -> int:
        """关闭所有会话（服务端退出或测试收尾时用）。"""
        sessions = list(self.sessions.values())
        for session in sessions:
            await session.close(reason, keep_reservation=False)
        return len(sessions)

    def error_for(self, session: "Session", code: str, message: str,
                  request_id: str | None = None) -> None:
        """给指定会话发一个错误帧（便捷方法）。"""
        session.send(error(code, message, request_id))
````

### `server/handlers.py`（424 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""M0 消息处理器：登录、房间、广播、私信、心跳。

新增一种消息类型 = 在 protocol.C2S_TYPES 里加名字 + 这里加一个 `@register` 函数。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from .protocol import ErrCode, error, make, now_ms, register


def _require_login(session) -> bool:
    """未登录时回错误并返回 False，供各处理器统一复用。"""
    if session.nickname is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.NOT_LOGGED_IN, "请先登录"))
        return False
    return True


def _text(data: dict, key: str = "text") -> str:
    """取并裁剪字符串字段，缺省返回空串。"""
    value = data.get(key)
    return value.strip() if isinstance(value, str) else ""


def _action_error(session, action, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """把引擎返回的 Action 翻译成 error 帧。"""
    code = getattr(action.code, "value", action.code) or ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE
    session.send(error(code, action.detail or "操作被拒绝", request_id))


def _table_of(session):
    """取会话所在牌桌；不在牌桌返回 None。"""
    room = session.hub.room_of(session)
    return room.table if room is not None else None


@register("login")
async def on_login(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """登录：账号密码交给 Hub 校验，成功后下发 welcome 并广播在线变化。"""
    if session.nickname is not None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, "你已经登录", request_id))
        return
    account = data.get("account") or data.get("nickname")
    password = data.get("password", "")
    if not isinstance(account, str) or not isinstance(password, str):
        session.send(error(ErrCode.INVALID_NICKNAME, "缺少 account 或 password", request_id))
        return

    ok, code, message = session.hub.login(session, account, password)
    if not ok:
        session.send(error(code or ErrCode.BAD_CREDENTIALS, message or "登录失败", request_id))
        return

    session.send(session.welcome_payload(request_id))
    room = session.hub.room_of(session)
    if room is not None:
        session.hub.broadcast_room_users(room)
        room.broadcast(
            make("user_joined", {"nickname": session.nickname, "room": room.name}),
            exclude=session,
        )
    session.hub.broadcast_room_list()


@register("register")
async def on_register(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """创建账号：输入账号与密码即可，成功后直接登录进大厅。"""
    if session.nickname is not None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, "你已经登录", request_id))
        return
    account = data.get("account")
    password = data.get("password", "")
    if not isinstance(account, str) or not isinstance(password, str):
        session.send(error(ErrCode.INVALID_NICKNAME, "缺少 account 或 password", request_id))
        return
    ok, code, message = session.hub.register(account, password)
    if not ok:
        session.send(error(code or ErrCode.INVALID_NICKNAME, message or "创建失败", request_id))
        return
    # 创建成功直接走登录流程（welcome 会带同一个 request_id 返回，客户端按登录处理）
    await on_login(session, {"account": account, "password": password}, request_id)


@register("list_conversations")
async def on_list_conversations(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """返回当前账号的私信会话摘要。"""
    if not _require_login(session):
        return
    session.send(make("conversations",
                      {"items": session.hub.conversations_of(session.nickname)}, request_id))


@register("get_history")
async def on_get_history(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """拉取与某人的私信历史（仅内存，服务端重启即清空）。"""
    if not _require_login(session):
        return
    peer = data.get("peer")
    if not isinstance(peer, str) or not peer:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, "缺少 peer", request_id))
        return
    session.send(make("history", {
        "peer": peer,
        "messages": session.hub.private_history(session.nickname, peer),
    }, request_id))


@register("logout")
async def on_logout(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """主动登出：先回执再断开（主动退出不保留重连预约）。"""
    session.send(make("left", {"reason": "logout"}, request_id))
    await session.close("logout", keep_reservation=False)


@register("resume")
async def on_resume(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """凭 token 恢复会话（握手阶段的第二个允许类型）。"""
    if session.nickname is not None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.ALREADY_LOGGED_IN, "当前连接已登录", request_id))
        return
    token = data.get("token")
    if not isinstance(token, str) or not token:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.SESSION_EXPIRED, "缺少 token", request_id))
        await session.close("resume without token", keep_reservation=False)
        return

    ok, code, message = session.hub.resume(session, token)
    if not ok:
        session.send(error(code or ErrCode.SESSION_EXPIRED, message or "恢复失败", request_id))
        await session.close("resume failed", keep_reservation=False)
        return

    session.send(session.resumed_payload(request_id))
    session.hub.broadcast_room_list()


@register("pong")
async def on_pong(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """客户端对服务端 ping 的回应；last_seen 已在读循环里刷新，这里无需额外处理。"""
    return


@register("ping")
async def on_ping(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """应用层心跳：回 pong 并带回 echo。"""
    session.send(make("pong", {"echo": data.get("echo")}, request_id))


@register("list_rooms")
async def on_list_rooms(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """返回房间列表。"""
    session.send(make("room_list", {"rooms": session.hub.room_list()}, request_id))


@register("list_users")
async def on_list_users(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """拉取在线玩家：既有本房间成员，也有全服在线账号（大厅的"刷新在线玩家"用）。"""
    room = session.hub.room_of(session)
    session.send(make("user_list", {
        "room": session.room,
        "users": room.nicknames() if room else [],
        "online": session.hub.online_accounts(),
    }, request_id))


@register("join_room")
async def on_join_room(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """加入房间：牌桌先做容量检查，再把角色（player/spectator）回给客户端。"""
    if not _require_login(session):
        return
    room_name = data.get("room")
    if not isinstance(room_name, str):
        session.send(error(ErrCode.ROOM_NOT_FOUND, "缺少 room", request_id))
        return
    target = session.hub.rooms.get(room_name)
    table = target.table if target is not None else None
    if table is not None:
        ok, code, message = table.can_join(session)
        if not ok:
            session.send(error(code or ErrCode.ROOM_FULL, message or "牌桌已满", request_id))
            return
    ok, code, message = session.hub.join(session, room_name)
    if not ok:
        session.send(error(code or ErrCode.ROOM_NOT_FOUND, message or "加入失败", request_id))
        return
    room = session.hub.room_of(session)
    role = "player"
    if table is not None:
        ok, code, message = table.join(session)
        if not ok:
            session.send(error(code or ErrCode.ROOM_FULL, message or "牌桌已满", request_id))
            return
        role = "player" if session.nickname in table.seats else "spectator"
        session.send(make("table_state", table.public_state()))
    session.send(make("joined", {"room": room_name, "users": room.nicknames() if room else [],
                                 "role": role}, request_id))


@register("leave_room")
async def on_leave_room(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """离开当前房间，回到大厅。"""
    if not _require_login(session):
        return
    previous = session.room
    table = _table_of(session)
    if table is not None:
        table.leave(session)
    ok, code, message = session.hub.leave(session)
    if not ok:
        session.send(error(code or ErrCode.NOT_IN_ROOM, message or "退出失败", request_id))
        return
    session.send(make("left", {"room": previous, "to": session.room}, request_id))


@register("create_group")
async def on_create_group(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """邀请在线用户创建群聊（被邀请者会被直接拉进群）。"""
    if not _require_login(session):
        return
    members = data.get("members")
    if not isinstance(members, list):
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, "members 必须是账号数组", request_id))
        return
    ok, code, message, room_name = session.hub.create_group(session, members)
    if not ok:
        session.send(error(code or ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, message or "建群失败", request_id))
        return
    room = session.hub.rooms[room_name]
    session.send(make("joined", {"room": room_name, "users": room.nicknames(),
                                 "role": "player", "owner": session.nickname}, request_id))


@register("delete_group")
async def on_delete_group(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """解散群聊（仅群主）。"""
    if not _require_login(session):
        return
    room_name = data.get("room") or session.room
    if not isinstance(room_name, str):
        session.send(error(ErrCode.GROUP_NOT_FOUND, "缺少 room", request_id))
        return
    ok, code, message = session.hub.delete_group(session, room_name)
    if not ok:
        session.send(error(code or ErrCode.GROUP_NOT_FOUND, message or "解散失败", request_id))
        return
    session.send(make("left", {"room": room_name, "to": session.room}, request_id))


@register("room_msg")
async def on_room_msg(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """房间聊天：广播给房间成员并给发送者回执。"""
    if not _require_login(session):
        return
    text = _text(data)
    if not text:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, "消息不能为空", request_id))
        return
    if len(text) > session.cfg.max_text_chars:
        session.send(
            error(
                ErrCode.MESSAGE_TOO_LONG,
                f"消息超过 {session.cfg.max_text_chars} 字符",
                request_id,
            )
        )
        return
    room = session.hub.room_of(session)
    if room is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.NOT_IN_ROOM, "你不在任何房间", request_id))
        return

    payload = make(
        "room_msg",
        {
            "room": room.name,
            "from": session.nickname,
            "text": text,
        },
    )
    delivered = room.broadcast(payload, droppable=True)
    session.send(make("room_msg_ack", {"delivered": delivered, "room": room.name}, request_id))


@register("private_msg")
async def on_private_msg(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """私信：投递给目标并记录到会话历史。"""
    if not _require_login(session):
        return
    target = data.get("to")
    text = _text(data)
    if not isinstance(target, str) or not target:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.USER_NOT_FOUND, "缺少收件人", request_id))
        return
    if not text:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, "消息不能为空", request_id))
        return
    if len(text) > session.cfg.max_text_chars:
        session.send(
            error(ErrCode.MESSAGE_TOO_LONG, f"消息超过 {session.cfg.max_text_chars} 字符", request_id)
        )
        return

    receiver = session.hub.find_by_nickname(target)
    if receiver is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.USER_NOT_FOUND, f"{target} 不在线", request_id))
        return

    payload = make("private_msg", {"from": session.nickname, "to": target, "text": text})
    receiver.send(payload)
    session.hub.record_private(session.nickname, target, text, now_ms())
    session.send(make("private_msg", {"from": session.nickname, "to": target, "text": text,
                                     "self": True}, request_id))


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 牌桌消息 ----


@register("room_ready")
async def on_room_ready(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """牌桌准备：三人齐且都准备时开局。"""
    table = _table_of(session)
    if table is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_STATE, "当前房间不是牌桌", request_id))
        return
    action = table.ready(session)
    if not action.ok:
        _action_error(session, action, request_id)
        return
    session.send(make("ok", {"action": "ready"}, request_id))


@register("bid")
async def on_bid(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """叫分：0 表示不叫，1~3 必须高于当前最高分。"""
    table = _table_of(session)
    if table is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_STATE, "当前房间不是牌桌", request_id))
        return
    score = data.get("score")
    if not isinstance(score, int) or score not in (0, 1, 2, 3):
        session.send(error(ErrCode.INVALID_BID, "叫分必须是 0/1/2/3", request_id))
        return
    action = table.bid(session, score, request_id)
    if not action.ok:
        _action_error(session, action, request_id)
        return
    session.send(make("ok", {"action": "bid", "score": score}, request_id))


@register("play")
async def on_play(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """出牌：cards 使用 '3s'/'BJ' 这类编码。"""
    table = _table_of(session)
    if table is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_STATE, "当前房间不是牌桌", request_id))
        return
    cards = data.get("cards")
    if not isinstance(cards, list) or not cards or not all(isinstance(c, str) for c in cards):
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_MESSAGE, "cards 必须是非空字符串数组", request_id))
        return
    action = table.play(session, cards, request_id)
    if not action.ok:
        _action_error(session, action, request_id)
        return
    session.send(make("ok", {"action": "play", "cards": cards}, request_id))


@register("pass")
async def on_pass(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """过牌。"""
    table = _table_of(session)
    if table is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_STATE, "当前房间不是牌桌", request_id))
        return
    action = table.pass_turn(session, request_id)
    if not action.ok:
        _action_error(session, action, request_id)
        return
    session.send(make("ok", {"action": "pass"}, request_id))


@register("game_state")
async def on_game_state(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """请求牌桌快照：玩家拿自己的手牌，观战者只拿公开信息。"""
    table = _table_of(session)
    if table is None or table.match is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.MATCH_NOT_STARTED, "牌桌还没有开始对局", request_id))
        return
    if session.nickname in table.seats:
        session.send(make("game_state", table.view_for(session.nickname), request_id))
    else:
        session.send(make("table_state", table.public_state(), request_id))


@register("spectate")
async def on_spectate(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """观战：加入牌桌但不占座位（座位未满时会提示直接入座）。"""
    room_name = data.get("room")
    if not isinstance(room_name, str) or room_name not in session.hub.rooms:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.ROOM_NOT_FOUND, "房间不存在", request_id))
        return
    room = session.hub.rooms[room_name]
    if room.table is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_STATE, "该房间不是牌桌", request_id))
        return
    if len(room.table.seats) < room.table.cfg.seats_per_table:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_STATE, "牌桌还有空位，请直接加入房间入座", request_id))
        return
    await on_join_room(session, {"room": room_name}, request_id)


@register("auto_play")
async def on_auto_play(session, data: dict, request_id: str | None) -> None:
    """在线托管开关：不下线也能把这一局交给服务端自动打，随时可以取回。"""
    table = _table_of(session)
    if table is None:
        session.send(error(ErrCode.BAD_STATE, "当前房间不是牌桌", request_id))
        return
    enabled = bool(data.get("enabled", True))
    action = table.set_auto(session, enabled)
    if not action.ok:
        _action_error(session, action, request_id)
        return
    session.send(make("ok", {"action": "auto_play", "enabled": enabled}, request_id))
````

### `tests/test_server.py`（232 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""服务端集成测试：登录、房间、广播、私信与异常输入。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

import pytest

from client.net import Connection, ServerError
from server.framing import read_frame
from server.protocol import ErrCode


async def test_login_and_welcome(server):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    welcome = await conn.login("alice")
    data = welcome["data"]
    assert data["nickname"] == "alice"
    assert data["room"] == "大厅"
    assert any(room["name"] == "聊天房" for room in data["rooms"])
    assert data["online"] == 1
    await conn.close()


async def test_duplicate_account_rejected(server):
    """同一账号不能在两个客户端同时登录。"""
    first = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await first.login("dup")
    second = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await second.login("dup")
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.ACCOUNT_LOGGED_IN
    await first.close()
    await second.close()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("nickname", ["", "   ", "a" * 40, "with space"])
async def test_invalid_nickname(server, nickname):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await conn.login(nickname)
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.INVALID_NICKNAME
    await conn.close()


async def test_first_message_must_be_login(server):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await conn.send("room_msg", {"text": "hi"})
    first = await conn.recv(2.0)
    assert first["type"] == "error"
    assert first["data"]["code"] == ErrCode.NOT_LOGGED_IN


async def test_ping_pong(server):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await conn.login("pinger")
    pong = await conn.ping()
    assert pong["type"] == "pong"
    await conn.close()


async def test_list_rooms(server):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await conn.login("lister")
    reply = await conn.list_rooms()
    names = [room["name"] for room in reply["data"]["rooms"]]
    assert "大厅" in names and "聊天房" in names
    await conn.close()


async def test_room_broadcast_and_isolation(server):
    a = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    b = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await a.login("roomA")
    await b.login("roomB")
    await a.join_room("聊天房")  # b 留在大厅

    await a.room_msg("只在聊天房可见")
    got = await a.wait_for("room_msg", match={"text": "只在聊天房可见"})
    assert got["data"]["from"] == "roomA"

    with pytest.raises(asyncio.TimeoutError):
        await b.wait_for("room_msg", timeout=0.3)
    await a.close()
    await b.close()


async def test_private_message_only_to_target(server):
    a = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    b = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    c = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    for conn, nick in ((a, "pmA"), (b, "pmB"), (c, "pmC")):
        await conn.login(nick)

    await a.private_msg("pmB", "悄悄话")
    got = await b.wait_for("private_msg", match={"text": "悄悄话"})
    assert got["data"]["from"] == "pmA"
    with pytest.raises(asyncio.TimeoutError):
        await c.wait_for("private_msg", timeout=0.3)
    for conn in (a, b, c):
        await conn.close()


async def test_private_message_to_offline_user(server):
    a = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await a.login("lonely")
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await a.private_msg("nobody", "在吗")
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.USER_NOT_FOUND
    await a.close()


async def test_leave_room_returns_to_lobby(server):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await conn.login("returner")
    await conn.join_room("聊天房")
    reply = await conn.leave_room()
    assert reply["data"]["to"] == "大厅"
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await conn.leave_room()  # 大厅不能退出
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.NOT_IN_ROOM
    await conn.close()


async def test_join_unknown_room(server):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await conn.login("explorer")
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await conn.join_room("不存在的房间")
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.ROOM_NOT_FOUND
    await conn.close()


async def test_room_users_updates(server):
    a = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await a.login("listA")
    await a.join_room("聊天房")
    b = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await b.login("listB")
    await b.join_room("聊天房")

    # 房间成员列表会随加入/退出多次推送，这里等到出现"两人都在"的那一条
    users: set[str] = set()
    deadline = asyncio.get_running_loop().time() + 3
    while asyncio.get_running_loop().time() < deadline:
        frame = await a.wait_for("room_users", timeout=1.0, match={"room": "聊天房"})
        users = set(frame["data"]["users"])
        if users == {"listA", "listB"}:
            break
    assert users == {"listA", "listB"}
    await a.close()
    await b.close()


async def test_message_too_long(server):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await conn.login("longy")
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await conn.room_msg("x" * (server.cfg.max_text_chars + 1))
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.MESSAGE_TOO_LONG
    await conn.close()


async def test_unknown_type_then_disconnect(server):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await conn.login("unknown")
    for _ in range(server.cfg.bad_frame_limit):
        await conn.send("nope_type", {})
    errors = []
    closed = False
    for _ in range(10):
        try:
            frame = await conn.recv(1.0)
        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
            break
        except (asyncio.IncompleteReadError, ConnectionError):
            closed = True
            break
        if frame.get("type") == "error":
            errors.append(frame)
    assert errors, "服务端应对未知类型回 error"
    assert errors[0]["data"]["code"] == ErrCode.UNKNOWN_TYPE
    assert "supported" in errors[0]["data"]
    assert closed, "连续未知类型后服务端应断开连接"
    await conn.close()


async def test_oversize_frame_rejected(server):
    """长度超限：服务端回 error 帧后必须断开（字节流已错位）。"""
    reader, writer = await asyncio.open_connection(server.host, server.port)
    writer.write((server.cfg.max_frame_bytes + 1).to_bytes(4, "big") + b"{")
    await writer.drain()
    payload = await asyncio.wait_for(read_frame(reader, 1 << 20), 2.0)
    assert payload["type"] == "error"
    assert payload["data"]["code"] == ErrCode.BAD_FRAME
    assert await asyncio.wait_for(reader.read(1), 2.0) == b"", "服务端应关闭连接"
    writer.close()


async def test_handshake_timeout(server_factory):
    server = await server_factory(handshake_timeout_ms=200)
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    frame = await conn.recv(2.0)  # 不登录，等服务端超时
    assert frame["type"] == "error"
    assert frame["data"]["code"] == ErrCode.HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT
    await conn.close()


async def test_prelogin_abrupt_disconnect_keeps_server_healthy(server):
    """回归：未登录连接断掉不能让服务端卡死（参照 Azuronis 的实测缺陷）。"""
    for _ in range(5):
        reader, writer = await asyncio.open_connection(server.host, server.port)
        writer.close()  # 连上就断，什么都不发
    await asyncio.sleep(0.2)

    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    welcome = await asyncio.wait_for(conn.login("healthy"), 2.0)
    assert welcome["data"]["nickname"] == "healthy"
    await conn.close()


async def test_disconnect_cleans_online_list(server):
    a = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await a.login("stay")
    b = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await b.login("leaver")
    await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    await b.close()
    await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
    assert server.online == 1
    assert "leaver" not in server.hub.by_nickname
    await a.close()
````

````python
"""账号体系与群聊：预置账号、唯一登录、登出、邀请建群与解散。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

import pytest

from client.net import Connection, ServerError
from server.protocol import ErrCode


PRESET = [f"wanjia{i}" for i in range(1, 6)]
PASSWORD = "123456"


# --------------------------------------------------------------- 账号 ----


@pytest.mark.parametrize("account", PRESET)
async def test_preset_accounts_can_login(server, account):
    conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    welcome = await conn.login(account, PASSWORD)
    assert welcome["data"]["account"] == account
    assert welcome["data"]["room"] == "大厅"

async def test_wrong_password_rejected(server):
````
