````python
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await conn.login("wanjia1", "wrong-password")
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.BAD_CREDENTIALS
    await conn.close()


async def test_same_account_cannot_login_twice(server):
    first = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await first.login("wanjia2", PASSWORD)

    second = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await second.login("wanjia2", PASSWORD)
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.ACCOUNT_LOGGED_IN
    assert "其它客户端" in exc.value.message

    # 第一个连接仍然可用（没有被顶掉）

    await first.close()

    await asyncio.sleep(0.2)

    second = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    welcome = await second.login("wanjia3", PASSWORD)
    assert welcome["data"]["account"] == "wanjia3"

async def test_auto_registered_account_keeps_its_password(server_factory):

async def test_register_rejects_duplicate_and_weak_password(server_factory):

async def test_private_history_and_conversations(server):
    """私信要能查到历史，并在会话列表里出现。"""
    alice = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await alice.login("wanjia1", PASSWORD)

    await bob.private_msg("wanjia1", "收到，这是回复")

    history = (await alice.request("get_history", {"peer": "wanjia2"}))["data"]["messages"]
    assert [m["text"] for m in history] == ["第一条私信", "收到，这是回复"]
    assert history[0]["from"] == "wanjia1"

    items = (await bob.request("list_conversations", {}))["data"]["items"]
    assert items and items[0]["peer"] == "wanjia1"
    assert items[0]["count"] == 2
    assert items[0]["last_text"] == "收到，这是回复"
    await alice.close()
    await bob.close()


async def test_create_group_invites_members(server):
    conns = []
    for account in ("wanjia1", "wanjia2", "wanjia3"):
        conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
        await conn.login(account, PASSWORD)
        await conn.join_room("聊天房")
        conns.append(conn)

    reply = await conns[0].request("create_group", {"members": ["wanjia2", "wanjia3"]})
    room = reply["data"]["room"]
    assert room.startswith("群聊")

    # 被邀请者也收到了 group_created，并且已经在群里
    for conn in conns:
        frame = await conn.wait_for("group_created", timeout=3.0)
        assert frame["data"]["room"] == room
        assert frame["data"]["owner"] == "wanjia1"
        assert set(frame["data"]["members"]) == {"wanjia1", "wanjia2", "wanjia3"}

    rooms = {r["name"]: r for r in server.hub.room_list()}
    assert rooms[room]["kind"] == "group"
    assert rooms[room]["owner"] == "wanjia1"
    assert rooms[room]["count"] == 3
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.close()

    await conn.login("wanjia4", PASSWORD)

    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.GROUP_MEMBER_OFFLINE
    await conn.close()


async def test_only_owner_can_dismiss_group(server):
    conns = []
    for account in ("wanjia1", "wanjia2"):
        conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
        await conn.login(account, PASSWORD)
        await conn.join_room("聊天房")
        conns.append(conn)

    reply = await conns[0].request("create_group", {"members": ["wanjia2"]})
    room = reply["data"]["room"]
    await asyncio.sleep(0.3)

    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await conns[1].request("delete_group", {"room": room})
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.NOT_GROUP_OWNER

    await conns[0].request("delete_group", {"room": room})
    await asyncio.sleep(0.3)
    names = [r["name"] for r in server.hub.room_list()]
    assert room not in names, "解散后房间应从列表里消失"
    assert server.hub.by_nickname["wanjia2"].room == "大厅", "成员应回到大厅"
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.close()
````

## 六、P0-4：牌桌、服务端裁决、手牌隐私与 request_id 幂等

**对应要求**

- 多人互动结果由服务端统一裁决。
- 客户端不能自己修改手牌、轮次或胜负。
- 重复 `request_id` 不得重复叫分、出牌或结算。
- 观战者只能收到公开状态，玩家只收到自己的手牌。

**功能与代码的对应关系**

- `game/engine.py`：对局状态机，检查轮次、手牌、牌型和压牌关系。
- `Match._cached()` / `_remember()`：按 `(玩家, request_id)` 实现幂等。
- `server/table.py`：把引擎接入网络，翻译事件并广播公开状态。
- `Table.public_state()`：观战者和公开广播使用。
- `Table.view_for()`：玩家视角。
- `Table._send_hand()`：手牌只发给本人。

**现场讲解结论**

客户端只能提交“叫分/出牌/过牌”的意图；服务端先检查是否轮到该玩家、牌是否
在手中、牌型是否合法、是否能压过桌面，然后才修改状态并广播结果。手牌走
`seat.send()` 的私有路径，不会进入公开状态。

### 本功能完整代码

### `game/engine.py`（475 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""对局状态机（本项目自研）。

一个 `Match` 实例就是"一局"：

    WAITING（等三人准备） → DEALING（发牌） → BIDDING（叫分）
        → PLAYING（出牌） → SETTLED（结算） → 可 reset 再来一局

设计约定：

1. **纯逻辑**：不涉及网络、asyncio、文件，只接收玩家的动作，返回结果与事件。
2. **时间由外部注入**：判定超时、生成截止时间都用调用方传入的时钟，
   保证"一切以服务端时间为准"，也方便测试用假时钟。
3. **事件流**：每次状态变更都追加事件，网络层的广播、观战推送、对局日志
   全部由事件生成，不需要另外维护一套逻辑。
4. **手牌私有**：只有 `view_for(玩家)` 才包含该玩家自己的手牌，
   `public_view()` 只给张数与公开信息（观战者用这个）。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import random
import time
from collections import Counter, OrderedDict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum

from .api import check_card_type, cards_greater, hand_str, new_game
from .cards import Card


class State(str, Enum):
    """对局阶段：等待/发牌/叫分/出牌/结算。"""
    WAITING = "WAITING"
    DEALING = "DEALING"
    BIDDING = "BIDDING"
    PLAYING = "PLAYING"
    SETTLED = "SETTLED"


class Err(str, Enum):
    """错误码，网络层直接把它放进 error 帧。"""

    BAD_STATE = "BAD_STATE"
    NOT_YOUR_TURN = "NOT_YOUR_TURN"
    ILLEGAL_BID = "ILLEGAL_BID"
    INVALID_PLAY = "INVALID_PLAY"
    NOT_IN_HAND = "NOT_IN_HAND"
    CANNOT_PASS = "CANNOT_PASS"
    NOT_ENOUGH_PLAYERS = "NOT_ENOUGH_PLAYERS"


@dataclass
class MatchConfig:
    """一局的可调参数（默认值取自大纲 v7）。"""

    bid_timeout_ms: int = 10_000
    play_timeout_ms: int = 20_000
    # 叫分/出牌超时设为 0 表示"不限时"（例如由真人自由出牌，交由网络层决定何时托管）
    max_redeal: int = 3          # 连续全不叫的次数上限，超过则本局作废
    base_score: int = 1
    request_cache_size: int = 256
    seat_count: int = 3


@dataclass
class Action:
    """动作的处理结果。"""

    ok: bool
    code: Err | None = None
    detail: str = ""
    duplicate: bool = False


@dataclass
class Match:
    """一局对局：从三人准备到结算的完整状态机（纯逻辑，不含网络）。"""
    players: list[str]
    seed: int | None = None
    config: MatchConfig = field(default_factory=MatchConfig)
    clock: object = None  # Callable[[], int]，毫秒

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """初始化一局的全部状态字段。"""
        self.cfg = self.config
        if self.clock is None:
            self.clock = lambda: int(time.monotonic() * 1000)  # noqa: E731
        if len(self.players) != self.cfg.seat_count:
            raise ValueError("当前只支持三人一桌")
        self.state = State.WAITING
        self.ready_set: set[str] = set()
        self.hands: dict[str, list[int]] = {p: [] for p in self.players}
        self.bottom: list[int] = []
        self.bottom_revealed = False
        self.landlord: str | None = None
        self.current: str | None = None
        self.bids: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.bid_order: list[str] = []
        self.table: list[int] = []
        self.table_owner: str | None = None
        self.passes = 0
        self.multiplier = 1
        self.bombs = 0
        self.play_counts: dict[str, int] = {p: 0 for p in self.players}
        self.history: list[dict] = []
        self.result: dict | None = None
        self.deadline_ms: int | None = None
        self.events: list[dict] = []
        self.deal_round = 0
        self._requests: OrderedDict[tuple[str, str], Action] = OrderedDict()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 查询 ----

    @property
    def landlord_side(self) -> list[str]:
        """地主之外的两名农民。"""
        return [p for p in self.players if p != self.landlord] if self.landlord else []

    def hand_counts(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        """各家剩余手牌数。"""
        return {p: len(h) for p, h in self.hands.items()}

    def _emit(self, **event) -> None:
        """追加一条带服务端时间戳的事件。"""
        event.setdefault("ts_ms", self.clock())
        event.setdefault("state", self.state.value)
        self.events.append(event)

    def _arm_deadline(self, timeout_ms: int) -> None:
        """设置本阶段截止时间；0 表示不限时。"""
        self.deadline_ms = self.clock() + timeout_ms if timeout_ms > 0 else None

    def drain_events(self) -> list[dict]:
        """取出并清空累计的事件（网络层据此广播）。"""
        events, self.events = self.events, []
        return events

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 生命周期 ----

    def ready(self, player: str) -> Action:
        """玩家准备；三人都准备好后自动发牌并进入叫分。"""
        if self.state is not State.WAITING:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, f"当前状态 {self.state.value} 不能准备")
        if player not in self.players:
            return Action(False, Err.NOT_ENOUGH_PLAYERS, f"{player} 不在本桌")
        self.ready_set.add(player)
        self._emit(type="ready", player=player, ready=len(self.ready_set))
        if len(self.ready_set) == len(self.players):
            self._deal()
        return Action(True)

    def _deal(self) -> None:
        """洗牌发牌：固定 seed 可复现，并确定第一个叫分的人。"""
        self.state = State.DEALING
        self.deal_round += 1
        deal_seed = None if self.seed is None else self.seed + self.deal_round - 1
        groups = new_game(deal_seed)
        self.hands = {p: groups[i] for i, p in enumerate(self.players)}
        self.bottom = groups[3]
        self.bottom_revealed = False
        self.table, self.table_owner, self.passes = [], None, 0
        self.bids, self.bid_order = {}, []
        self.multiplier = 1
        self.bombs = 0
        self.play_counts = {p: 0 for p in self.players}
        self.landlord = None
        self.result = None
        self._emit(type="dealt", deal_round=self.deal_round, hand_counts=self.hand_counts())

        first = random.Random(deal_seed if deal_seed is not None else self.clock()).randrange(
            len(self.players)
        )
        self.bid_order = self.players[first:] + self.players[:first]
        self.current = self.bid_order[0]
        self.state = State.BIDDING
        self._arm_deadline(self.cfg.bid_timeout_ms)
        self._emit(type="bid_start", first=self.current, order=list(self.bid_order))

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """一局结束后复位，可直接再开一局。"""
        self.__post_init__()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 叫分 ----

    def bid(self, player: str, score: int, request_id: str | None = None) -> Action:
        """叫分：0 表示不叫，1/2/3 必须高于当前最高分。"""
        cached = self._cached(player, request_id)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
        if self.state is not State.BIDDING:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, f"当前状态 {self.state.value} 不能叫分")
        if player != self.current:
            return Action(False, Err.NOT_YOUR_TURN, f"现在轮到 {self.current}")
        if score not in (0, 1, 2, 3):
            return Action(False, Err.ILLEGAL_BID, "叫分只能是 0/1/2/3")
        highest = max(self.bids.values(), default=0)
        if score != 0 and score <= highest:
            return Action(False, Err.ILLEGAL_BID, f"叫分必须高于当前最高分 {highest}")

        self.bids[player] = score
        self._emit(type="bid", player=player, score=score)
        self._remember(player, request_id, Action(True))

        if score == 3 or len(self.bids) == len(self.players):
            self._decide_landlord()
            return Action(True)

        self._next_bidder()
        return Action(True)

    def _next_bidder(self) -> None:
        remaining = [p for p in self.bid_order if p not in self.bids]
        if not remaining:
            self._decide_landlord()
            return
        self.current = remaining[0]
        self._arm_deadline(self.cfg.bid_timeout_ms)

    def _decide_landlord(self) -> None:
        best = max(self.bids.values(), default=0)
        if best == 0:
            if self.deal_round >= self.cfg.max_redeal:
                self._emit(type="match_aborted", reason="无人叫分", deal_round=self.deal_round)
                self.state = State.WAITING
                self.ready_set.clear()
                self.deadline_ms = None
                return
            self._emit(type="redeal", reason="三家都不叫")
            self._deal()
            return

        self.landlord = next(p for p in self.bid_order if self.bids.get(p) == best)
        self.multiplier = best
        self.bottom_revealed = True
        self.hands[self.landlord] = Card.sort_cards_by_rank_int(
            self.hands[self.landlord] + self.bottom
        )
        self.state = State.PLAYING
        self.current = self.landlord
        self.table, self.table_owner, self.passes = [], None, 0
        self._arm_deadline(self.cfg.play_timeout_ms)
        self._emit(
            type="landlord_decided",
            landlord=self.landlord,
            bid=best,
            bottom=list(self.bottom),
            hand_counts=self.hand_counts(),
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 出牌 ----

    def play(self, player: str, cards: list[int], request_id: str | None = None) -> Action:
        cached = self._cached(player, request_id)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
        if self.state is not State.PLAYING:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, f"当前状态 {self.state.value} 不能出牌")
        if player != self.current:
            return Action(False, Err.NOT_YOUR_TURN, f"现在轮到 {self.current}")
        if not cards:
            return Action(False, Err.INVALID_PLAY, "没有出牌")
        if Counter(cards) - Counter(self.hands[player]):
            return Action(False, Err.NOT_IN_HAND, "出的牌不在手牌里")

        ok, info = check_card_type(cards)
        if not ok:
            return Action(False, Err.INVALID_PLAY, f"牌型不合法：{info}")
        if self.table:
            greater, _ = cards_greater(cards, self.table)
            if not greater:
                return Action(False, Err.INVALID_PLAY, f"压不过 {hand_str(self.table)}")

        self._apply_play(player, cards)
        self._remember(player, request_id, Action(True))
        return Action(True)

    def pass_turn(self, player: str, request_id: str | None = None) -> Action:
        """过牌；只有在别人出过牌（桌面非空）时才能过。"""
        cached = self._cached(player, request_id)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
        if self.state is not State.PLAYING:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, f"当前状态 {self.state.value} 不能过牌")
        if player != self.current:
            return Action(False, Err.NOT_YOUR_TURN, f"现在轮到 {self.current}")
        if not self.table:
            return Action(False, Err.CANNOT_PASS, "你是本轮先手，必须出牌")

        self._apply_pass(player)
        self._remember(player, request_id, Action(True))
        return Action(True)

    def _apply_play(self, player: str, cards: list[int]) -> None:
        hand = list(self.hands[player])
        for card in cards:
            hand.remove(card)
        self.hands[player] = hand
        self.play_counts[player] += 1
        self.table = Card.sort_cards_by_rank_int(cards)
        self.table_owner = player
        self.passes = 0
        self.history.append({"event": "play", "player": player, "cards": list(self.table)})

        is_bomb = len(cards) == 4 and len({Card.get_rank_int(c) for c in cards}) == 1
        is_rocket = len(cards) == 2 and all(Card.get_rank_int(c) >= 13 for c in cards)
        if is_bomb or is_rocket:
            self.bombs += 1
            self.multiplier *= 2
        self._emit(
            type="play",
            player=player,
            cards=list(self.table),
            cards_text=hand_str(self.table),
            hand_count=len(hand),
            bomb=is_bomb or is_rocket,
            multiplier=self.multiplier,
        )

        if not hand:
            self._settle(player)
            return
        self._advance(player)

    def _apply_pass(self, player: str) -> None:
        self.passes += 1
        self.history.append({"event": "pass", "player": player})
        self._emit(type="pass", player=player, passes=self.passes)
        if self.passes >= 2:
            self.table, self.table_owner, self.passes = [], None, 0
            self.current = self._last_player(player)
            self._emit(type="table_cleared", leader=self.current)
        else:
            self._advance(player)
        self._arm_deadline(self.cfg.play_timeout_ms)

    def _advance(self, player: str) -> None:
        idx = self.players.index(player)
        self.current = self.players[(idx + 1) % len(self.players)]
        self._arm_deadline(self.cfg.play_timeout_ms)

    def _last_player(self, fallback: str) -> str:
        """最近一次真正出牌的玩家（用于两度过牌后重新领出）。"""
        for item in reversed(self.history):
            if item["event"] == "play":
                return item["player"]
        return fallback

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 超时 ----

    def tick(self, now_ms: int | None = None) -> Action | None:
        """由服务端定时调用：到点就替当前玩家做动作。"""
        if self.deadline_ms is None or self.state not in (State.BIDDING, State.PLAYING):
            return None
        now = self.clock() if now_ms is None else now_ms
        if now < self.deadline_ms:
            return None
        return self.timeout()

    def timeout(self) -> Action:
        """当前玩家超时：叫分视为不叫；跟牌视为过牌；先手自动出最小单张。"""
        player = self.current
        if player is None:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, "没有当前玩家")
        if self.state is State.BIDDING:
            self._emit(type="timeout", player=player, phase="BIDDING", action="不叫")
            return self.bid(player, 0)
        if self.state is State.PLAYING:
            if self.table:
                self._emit(type="timeout", player=player, phase="PLAYING", action="过牌")
                return self.pass_turn(player)
            smallest = min(self.hands[player], key=Card.get_rank_int)
            self._emit(type="timeout", player=player, phase="PLAYING", action="自动出最小单张")
            return self.play(player, [smallest])
        return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, f"当前状态 {self.state.value} 无超时处理")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 结算 ----

    def _settle(self, winner: str) -> None:
        """结算：判断春天、计算倍数与零和分数。"""
        landlord_win = winner == self.landlord
        spring = landlord_win and all(
            self.play_counts[p] == 0 for p in self.landlord_side
        )
        if spring:
            self.multiplier *= 2
        total = self.cfg.base_score * self.multiplier
        # 零和结算：地主 ±2×总分，两个农民各 ∓总分，三人相加恒为 0。
        scores = {p: -total for p in self.players}
        scores[self.landlord] = -2 * total
        if landlord_win:
            scores[self.landlord] = 2 * total
            for p in self.landlord_side:
                scores[p] = -total
        else:
            for p in self.landlord_side:
                scores[p] = total

        self.state = State.SETTLED
        self.deadline_ms = None
        self.result = {
            "winner": winner,
            "landlord": self.landlord,
            "landlord_win": landlord_win,
            "spring": spring,
            "bombs": self.bombs,
            "multiplier": self.multiplier,
            "scores": scores,
        }
        self._emit(
            type="game_over",
            winner=winner,
            landlord=self.landlord,
            landlord_win=landlord_win,
            spring=spring,
            bombs=self.bombs,
            multiplier=self.multiplier,
            scores=scores,
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 视图 ----

    def view_for(self, player: str | None) -> dict:
        """给某个玩家的视图；player 为 None 时返回观战视图（不含任何手牌）。"""
        view = self.public_view()
        if player in self.players:
            view["you"] = player
            view["hand"] = list(self.hands[player])
            view["hand_text"] = hand_str(self.hands[player])
        return view

    def public_view(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "state": self.state.value,
            "players": list(self.players),
            "landlord": self.landlord,
            "current": self.current,
            "hand_counts": self.hand_counts(),
            "bottom": list(self.bottom) if self.bottom_revealed else [],
            "bottom_revealed": self.bottom_revealed,
            "table": list(self.table),
            "table_owner": self.table_owner,
            "bids": dict(self.bids),
            "multiplier": self.multiplier,
            "bombs": self.bombs,
            "deadline_ms": self.deadline_ms,
            "result": self.result,
            "deal_round": self.deal_round,
            "history_len": len(self.history),
        }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 幂等 ----

    def _cached(self, player: str, request_id: str | None) -> Action | None:
        """幂等作用域是"每个玩家各自的 request_id"。

        不能用全局 request_id 去重：不同客户端的编号会重号，
        别人用过的编号会把自己的动作当成重复请求吞掉（实测踩到过这个坑）。
        """
        if not request_id:
            return None
        key = (player, request_id)
        hit = self._requests.get(key)
        if hit is None:
            return None
        self._requests.move_to_end(key)
        return Action(ok=hit.ok, code=hit.code, detail=hit.detail, duplicate=True)

    def _remember(self, player: str, request_id: str | None, action: Action) -> None:
        if not request_id:
            return
        key = (player, request_id)
        self._requests[key] = action
        self._requests.move_to_end(key)
        while len(self._requests) > self.cfg.request_cache_size:
            self._requests.popitem(last=False)
````

### `server/table.py`（389 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""牌桌：把斗地主对局引擎（game.engine.Match）接到网络层。

设计要点：

* 牌桌是"房间"的一种（`room.kind == "doudizhu"`），坐在座位上的是玩家，其余是观战者；
* 对局引擎完全不知道网络的存在：这里只做三件事——把玩家动作转成引擎调用、
  把引擎事件翻译成协议消息、按需下发私有视图（手牌只发给本人）；
* 断线不立刻放弃座位：标记为托管，轮到他时由服务端自动决策（默认延迟 1 秒）；
  凭 token 重连即可拿回手牌继续打。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from game.cards import Card
from game.engine import Action, Err, Match, MatchConfig, State
from game.text import card_code, cards_text, parse_card

from .protocol import ErrCode, error, make, now_ms

if TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover
    from .session import Session


class Table:
    kind = "doudizhu"

    def __init__(self, room, cfg) -> None:
        """牌桌状态：座位表（账号→会话，None 表示断线托管）、观战者与对局实例。"""
        self.room = room
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.seats: dict[str, "Session | None"] = {}      # 昵称 -> 会话（None 表示断线托管中）
        self.spectators: dict[str, "Session"] = {}        # conn_id -> 会话
        self.match: Match | None = None
        self.ready_set: set[str] = set()
        self.auto_players: set[str] = set()   # 在线托管：没掉线但交给服务端自动打
        self._last_turn: str | None = None
        self._turn_started_ms = now_ms()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 座位 ----

    def seat_names(self) -> list[str]:
        """按座位顺序返回账号列表。"""
        return list(self.seats)

    def can_join(self, session: "Session") -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None]:
        """只做容量判断，不改变状态（供加入房间前检查）。"""
        nickname = session.nickname
        if nickname in self.seats:
            return True, None, None
        if len(self.seats) < self.cfg.seats_per_table:
            return True, None, None
        if len(self.spectators) < self.cfg.max_spectators:
            return True, None, None
        return False, ErrCode.ROOM_FULL, "牌桌已满（3 人 + 2 观战）"

    def session_of(self, session: "Session") -> str | None:
        """根据连接找到对应座位账号；观战者返回 None。"""
        for nickname, seat in self.seats.items():
            if seat is session:
                return nickname
        return None

    def join(self, session: "Session") -> tuple[bool, str | None, str | None]:
        """就座或观战。返回 (是否成功, 错误码, 说明)。"""
        nickname = session.nickname
        ok, code, message = self.can_join(session)
        if not ok:
            return False, code, message
        if nickname in self.seats:  # 重连后重新入座
            self.seats[nickname] = session
            return True, None, None
        if len(self.seats) < self.cfg.seats_per_table:
            self.seats[nickname] = session
            self._ensure_match()
            self.broadcast(make("table_state", self.public_state()), droppable=True)
            return True, None, None
        if len(self.spectators) < self.cfg.max_spectators:
            self.spectators[session.conn_id] = session
            session.send(make("spectating", {"room": self.room.name,
                                             "state": self.public_state()}))
            if self.match is not None:
                session.send(make("table_state", self.public_state()))
            return True, None, None
        return False, ErrCode.ROOM_FULL, "牌桌已满（3 人 + 2 观战）"

    def leave(self, session: "Session") -> None:
        """离开牌桌：清座位/观战位并广播最新状态。"""
        nickname = self.session_of(session)
        if nickname is not None:
            self.seats.pop(nickname, None)
            self.ready_set.discard(nickname)
            if self.match is not None:
                self.broadcast(make("table_state", self.public_state()), droppable=True)
        self.spectators.pop(session.conn_id, None)

    def on_disconnect(self, session: "Session") -> None:
        """掉线：保留座位，标记为托管中。"""
        nickname = self.session_of(session)
        if nickname is not None:
            self.seats[nickname] = None
            self.auto_players.add(nickname)
            self.room.broadcast(
                make("table_state", self.public_state() | {"auto": nickname}), droppable=True
            )
        self.spectators.pop(session.conn_id, None)

    def on_resume(self, session: "Session") -> bool:
        """玩家重连：把会话重新绑定到座位，并补发私有对局快照。"""
        nickname = session.nickname
        if nickname not in self.seats:
            return False
        self.seats[nickname] = session
        self.auto_players.discard(nickname)   # 回来即取回控制
        if self.match is not None:
            session.send(make("game_state", self.view_for(nickname)))
        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 对局 ----

    def _ensure_match(self) -> None:
        """座位坐满时才创建对局；开局由 ready() 在三人全部准备后触发。"""
        if self.match is None and len(self.seats) == self.cfg.seats_per_table:
            # 引擎层不设超时：真人出牌不限时，AI/托管由本类的 tick() 按 auto_play_delay_ms 驱动
            self.match = Match(
                list(self.seats),
                seed=None,
                config=MatchConfig(bid_timeout_ms=0, play_timeout_ms=0),
            )
            self._last_turn = None

    def ready(self, session: "Session") -> Action:
        """准备：三人齐且都准备时开局；上一局已结算则清空后重开。"""
        nickname = self.session_of(session)
        if nickname is None:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, "你不在牌桌座位上")
        # 上一局已结算：清掉旧对局，等三家重新准备再开新一局
        if self.match is not None and self.match.state is State.SETTLED:
            self.match = None
            self.ready_set = set()
            self._last_turn = None
        if self.match is not None and self.match.state is not State.WAITING:
            return Action(True)  # 已经开局，重复准备视为成功
        self.ready_set.add(nickname)
        self._ensure_match()
        if self.match is None:
            # 人数不足：先记下准备状态，等第三位入座并准备后自动开局
            self.broadcast(
                make("table_state", self.public_state() | {"ready": sorted(self.ready_set)}),
                droppable=True,
            )
            return Action(True)
        if len(self.ready_set) < self.cfg.seats_per_table:
            self.broadcast(
                make("table_state", self.public_state() | {"ready": sorted(self.ready_set)}),
                droppable=True,
            )
            return Action(True)

        # 三家都准备：同时置位，Match 内部随即发牌
        for seat_nickname in self.seats:
            action = self.match.ready(seat_nickname)
            if not action.ok:
                return action
        self._flush_events()
        return Action(True)

    def set_auto(self, session: "Session", enabled: bool) -> Action:
        """在线托管开关：托管期间服务端替这位玩家决策，随时可取回。"""
        nickname = self.session_of(session)
        if nickname is None:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, "你不在牌桌座位上")
        if enabled:
            self.auto_players.add(nickname)
        else:
            self.auto_players.discard(nickname)
        self.broadcast(make("table_state", self.public_state()), droppable=True)
        return Action(True)

    def bid(self, session: "Session", score: int, request_id: str | None) -> Action:
        """叫分：交给引擎判定，并把产生的事件翻译广播。"""
        nickname = self.session_of(session)
        if nickname is None or self.match is None:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, "对局尚未开始")
        action = self.match.bid(nickname, score, request_id)
        if action.ok:
            self._flush_events()
            self.broadcast(make("table_state", self.public_state()), droppable=True)
        return action

    def play(self, session: "Session", card_texts: list[str],
             request_id: str | None) -> Action:
        """出牌：把协议里的 '3s' 编码还原成整数牌，交给引擎校验。"""
        nickname = self.session_of(session)
        if nickname is None or self.match is None:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, "对局尚未开始")
        try:
            cards = [parse_card(text) for text in card_texts]
        except (KeyError, IndexError):
            return Action(False, Err.INVALID_PLAY, f"无法识别的牌: {card_texts}")
        action = self.match.play(nickname, cards, request_id)
        if action.ok:
            self._flush_events()
            self.broadcast(make("table_state", self.public_state()), droppable=True)
        return action

    def pass_turn(self, session: "Session", request_id: str | None) -> Action:
        """过牌：交给引擎判定并广播结果。"""
        nickname = self.session_of(session)
        if nickname is None or self.match is None:
            return Action(False, Err.BAD_STATE, "对局尚未开始")
        action = self.match.pass_turn(nickname, request_id)
        if action.ok:
            self._flush_events()
            self.broadcast(make("table_state", self.public_state()), droppable=True)
        return action

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 定时 ----

    async def tick(self, now: int | None = None) -> None:
        """由服务端扫描任务驱动：超时与断线托管都在这里发生。"""
        now = now or now_ms()
        if self.match is None:
            return
        current = self.match.current
        if current != self._last_turn:
            self._last_turn = current
            self._turn_started_ms = now

        if self.match.state in (State.BIDDING, State.PLAYING):
            seated = self.seats.get(current) if current else None
            auto = seated is None or current in self.auto_players
            # 只有"托管/掉线"才由服务端代打；真人自由出牌，不设时限
            if auto and now - self._turn_started_ms >= self.cfg.auto_play_delay_ms:
                self.match.timeout()
                self._flush_events()
                self.broadcast(make("table_state", self.public_state()), droppable=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 视图 ----

    def public_state(self) -> dict:
        """公开视图：状态、轮次、各家张数、桌面牌、倍数与剩余时间（观战用）。"""
        if self.match is None:
            # 未开局也给出完整的公开字段，客户端不必做防御式解析
            return {
                "room": self.room.name,
                "state": "WAITING",
                "players": list(self.seats),
                "landlord": None,
                "current": None,
                "hand_counts": {nickname: 0 for nickname in self.seats},
                "bottom": [],
                "bottom_revealed": False,
                "table": [],
                "table_owner": None,
                "bids": {},
                "multiplier": 1,
                "bombs": 0,
                "deadline_ms": None,
                "result": None,
                "deal_round": 0,
                "history_len": 0,
                "seats": [{"nickname": n, "online": self.seats[n] is not None}
                          for n in self.seats],
                "spectators": len(self.spectators),
            }
        view = self.match.public_view()
        # 牌统一用协议编码（'3s'），整数表示只在服务端内部使用
        view["table"] = [card_code(c) for c in view.get("table", [])]
        view["table_text"] = cards_text(self.match.table)
        view["bottom"] = [card_code(c) for c in view.get("bottom", [])]
        view["bottom_text"] = cards_text(self.match.bottom) if view.get("bottom_revealed") else ""
        # 客户端不共享服务端时钟：托管席位给出"还有多久自动出牌"，真人席位不限时
        current = view.get("current")
        auto = current in self.auto_players or (
            current is not None and self.seats.get(current) is None
        )
        if auto and self.match.state in (State.BIDDING, State.PLAYING):
            elapsed = now_ms() - self._turn_started_ms
            view["deadline_in_ms"] = max(0, self.cfg.auto_play_delay_ms - elapsed)
        else:
            view["deadline_in_ms"] = None
        view["room"] = self.room.name
        view["seats"] = [{"nickname": n, "online": self.seats.get(n) is not None}
                         for n in self.seats]
        view["auto_players"] = sorted(self.auto_players)
        view["spectators"] = len(self.spectators)
        return view

    def view_for(self, nickname: str) -> dict:
        """协议视图：手牌用 '3s' 这类字符串编码（整数表示只在服务端内部使用）。"""
        assert self.match is not None
        raw = self.match.view_for(nickname)
        hand = list(raw.pop("hand", []))
        view = dict(raw)
        view["room"] = self.room.name
        view["hand"] = [card_code(c) for c in hand]
        view["hand_text"] = cards_text(hand)
        view["seats"] = [{"nickname": n, "online": self.seats.get(n) is not None}
                         for n in self.seats]
        return view

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 推送 ----

    def broadcast(self, payload: dict, droppable: bool = False) -> None:
        """把一帧发给本桌所有玩家与观战者。"""
        for seat in list(self.seats.values()):
            if seat is not None:
                seat.send(payload, droppable=droppable)
        for spectator in list(self.spectators.values()):
            spectator.send(payload, droppable=droppable)

    def _flush_events(self) -> None:
        """把引擎事件翻译成协议消息广播出去；手牌只发给本人。"""
        assert self.match is not None
        for event in self.match.drain_events():
            kind = event["type"]
            if kind == "bid_start":
                continue
            if kind == "dealt":
                # 发牌完成：把 17 张手牌分别发给本人
                for nickname, seat in self.seats.items():
                    if seat is not None:
                        self._send_hand(nickname, "game_start")
                self.broadcast(make("table_state", self.public_state()), droppable=True)
                continue
            if kind == "bid":
                self.broadcast(make("bid", {"player": event["player"], "score": event["score"]}))
            elif kind == "redeal":
                self.broadcast(make("rule", {"event": "redeal"}))
            elif kind == "match_aborted":
                self.broadcast(make("game_over", {"aborted": True, "reason": event["reason"]}))
                self.ready_set.clear()
            elif kind == "landlord_decided":
                self.broadcast(make("landlord", {
                    "landlord": event["landlord"],
                    "bid": event["bid"],
                    "bottom": [card_code(c) for c in event["bottom"]],
                    "bottom_text": cards_text(event["bottom"]),
                    "hand_counts": event["hand_counts"],
                }))
                landlord = event["landlord"]
                self._send_hand(landlord)
            elif kind == "play":
                self.broadcast(make("play", {
                    "player": event["player"],
                    "cards": [card_code(c) for c in event["cards"]],
                    "cards_text": event["cards_text"],
                    "hand_count": event["hand_count"],
                    "bomb": event["bomb"],
                    "multiplier": event["multiplier"],
                }))
            elif kind == "pass":
                self.broadcast(make("pass", {"player": event["player"]}))
            elif kind == "table_cleared":
                self.broadcast(make("table_cleared", {"leader": event["leader"]}))
            elif kind == "timeout":
                self.broadcast(make("timeout", {
                    "player": event["player"], "phase": event["phase"],
                    "action": event["action"],
                }))
            elif kind == "game_over":
                self.broadcast(make("game_over", {
                    "winner": event["winner"],
                    "landlord": event["landlord"],
                    "landlord_win": event["landlord_win"],
                    "spring": event["spring"],
                    "bombs": event["bombs"],
                    "multiplier": event["multiplier"],
                    "scores": event["scores"],
                }))
                self.ready_set.clear()

    def _send_hand(self, nickname: str, kind: str = "hand") -> None:
        """给某个玩家发私有手牌（game_start=发牌，hand=拿底牌）。"""
        seat = self.seats.get(nickname)
        if seat is None or self.match is None:
            return
        view = self.view_for(nickname)
        seat.send(make(kind, {
            "room": self.room.name,
            "hand": view.get("hand", []),
            "hand_text": view.get("hand_text", ""),
            "landlord": view.get("landlord"),
            "current": view.get("current"),
            "state": view.get("state"),
            "history_len": view.get("history_len"),
        }))
````

### `tests/test_engine.py`（277 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""对局状态机测试（自研部分的验收依据）。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import game
from game import Err, Match, MatchConfig, State
from game.cards import Card


class FakeClock:
    def __init__(self, now: int = 0) -> None:
        self.now = now

    def __call__(self) -> int:
        return self.now

    def advance(self, ms: int) -> None:
        self.now += ms


def new_match(seed: int = 20261011, clock=None) -> Match:
    match = Match(["p0", "p1", "p2"], seed=seed, clock=clock)
    for player in match.players:
        assert match.ready(player).ok
    return match


def to_playing(match: Match, bidder: str = None, score: int = 3) -> Match:
    """把对局推进到出牌阶段，默认由当前叫分者直接叫 3 分当上地主。"""
    bidder = bidder or match.current
    assert match.bid(bidder, score).ok
    if match.state is State.PLAYING:
        return match
    # 叫 3 分必然立即定地主，这里不该走到
    raise AssertionError(f"未进入出牌阶段: {match.state}")


# --------------------------------------------------------------- 发牌 ----


def test_deal_and_bid_order():
    match = new_match()
    assert match.state is State.BIDDING
    assert match.hand_counts() == {"p0": 17, "p1": 17, "p2": 17}
    assert len(match.bottom) == 3
    assert sorted(match.bid_order) == match.players
    assert match.current == match.bid_order[0]


def test_same_seed_same_deal():
    a = new_match(seed=42)
    b = new_match(seed=42)
    assert a.hands == b.hands
    assert a.bottom == b.bottom


# --------------------------------------------------------------- 叫分 ----


def test_bid_three_makes_landlord_immediately():
    match = new_match()
    first = match.current
    assert match.bid(first, 3).ok
    assert match.state is State.PLAYING
    assert match.landlord == first
    assert match.multiplier == 3
    assert match.bottom_revealed is True
    assert match.hand_counts()[first] == 20, "地主应拿到三张底牌"
    assert match.current == first, "地主先出牌"


def test_bid_must_exceed_highest():
    match = new_match()
    assert match.bid(match.current, 2).ok
    second = match.current
    assert match.bid(second, 1).code is Err.ILLEGAL_BID
    assert match.bid(second, 2).code is Err.ILLEGAL_BID
    assert match.bid(second, 3).ok
    assert match.landlord == second


def test_all_pass_redeals_then_aborts():
    match = new_match()
    match.drain_events()
    for _ in range(3):
        while match.state is State.BIDDING:
            assert match.bid(match.current, 0).ok
    assert match.state is State.WAITING, "连续三次全不叫后本局作废"
    assert match.deal_round == 3
    events = match.drain_events()
    assert sum(1 for e in events if e["type"] == "redeal") == 2, "前两次全不叫应重发"
    assert any(e["type"] == "match_aborted" for e in events)


# --------------------------------------------------------------- 出牌 ----


def test_not_your_turn():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    landlord = match.landlord
    other = next(p for p in match.players if p != landlord)
    card = match.hands[other][0]
    assert match.play(other, [card]).code is Err.NOT_YOUR_TURN


def test_play_card_not_in_hand():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    landlord = match.landlord
    own = set(match.hands[landlord])
    foreign = next(c for c in game.ALL_CARDS if c not in own)
    assert match.play(landlord, [foreign]).code is Err.NOT_IN_HAND


def test_play_invalid_type():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    landlord = match.landlord
    hand = match.hands[landlord]
    # 取两张点数不同的牌，凑不成任何牌型
    by_rank: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
    for card in hand:
        by_rank.setdefault(Card.get_rank_int(card), []).append(card)
    singles = [cards[0] for cards in by_rank.values()]
    assert match.play(landlord, singles[:2]).code is Err.INVALID_PLAY


def test_follow_must_beat():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    landlord = match.landlord
    others = [p for p in match.players if p != landlord]
    smallest = min(match.hands[landlord], key=Card.get_rank_int)
    assert match.play(landlord, [smallest]).ok

    follower = match.current
    lower = [c for c in match.hands[follower] if Card.get_rank_int(c) < Card.get_rank_int(smallest)]
    if lower:
        assert match.play(follower, [lower[0]]).code is Err.INVALID_PLAY


def test_cannot_pass_when_leading():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    assert match.pass_turn(match.current).code is Err.CANNOT_PASS


def test_two_passes_clear_table_and_leader_leads():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    leader = match.landlord
    card = min(match.hands[leader], key=Card.get_rank_int)
    assert match.play(leader, [card]).ok
    for _ in range(2):
        assert match.pass_turn(match.current).ok
    assert match.table == []
    assert match.current == leader, "两度过牌后由最后出牌者重新领出"


# --------------------------------------------------------------- 结算 ----


def test_win_settles_and_scores_are_zero_sum():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    landlord = match.landlord
    match.hands[landlord] = [Card.new("3s")]  # 直接构造必胜局面
    assert match.play(landlord, [Card.new("3s")]).ok
    assert match.state is State.SETTLED
    result = match.result
    assert result["landlord_win"] is True
    assert sum(result["scores"].values()) == 0
    total = result["multiplier"]
    assert result["scores"][landlord] == 2 * total
    for farmer in match.players:
        if farmer != landlord:
            assert result["scores"][farmer] == -total


def test_spring_doubles_multiplier():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    landlord = match.landlord
    base_multiplier = match.multiplier
    match.hands[landlord] = [Card.new("3s")]
    assert match.play(landlord, [Card.new("3s")]).ok
    assert match.result["spring"] is True
    assert match.result["multiplier"] == base_multiplier * 2


def test_bomb_doubles_multiplier():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    landlord = match.landlord
    match.hands[landlord] = [Card.new(f"3{s}") for s in "shdc"]
    before = match.multiplier
    assert match.play(landlord, list(match.hands[landlord])).ok
    assert match.result["bombs"] == 1
    # 炸弹 ×2，且地主一轮走完（农民没出过牌）还要再 ×2 春天
    assert match.result["multiplier"] == before * 2 * 2


# --------------------------------------------------------------- 超时 ----


def test_timeout_on_bidding_counts_as_pass():
    clock = FakeClock()
    match = new_match(clock=clock)
    pending = match.current
    clock.advance(MatchConfig().bid_timeout_ms + 1)
    action = match.tick()
    assert action is not None and action.ok
    assert match.bids[pending] == 0
    assert any(e["type"] == "timeout" and e["player"] == pending for e in match.drain_events())


def test_timeout_on_follow_passes():
    clock = FakeClock()
    match = to_playing(new_match(clock=clock))
    landlord = match.landlord
    card = min(match.hands[landlord], key=Card.get_rank_int)
    assert match.play(landlord, [card]).ok
    follower = match.current
    match.drain_events()
    clock.advance(MatchConfig().play_timeout_ms + 1)
    assert match.tick().ok
    assert any(e["type"] == "pass" and e["player"] == follower for e in match.drain_events() or [])


def test_timeout_when_leading_plays_smallest_single():
    clock = FakeClock()
    match = to_playing(new_match(clock=clock))
    landlord = match.landlord
    smallest = min(match.hands[landlord], key=Card.get_rank_int)
    match.drain_events()
    clock.advance(MatchConfig().play_timeout_ms + 1)
    assert match.tick().ok
    assert match.table == [smallest]
    assert match.play_counts[landlord] == 1


# --------------------------------------------------------------- 幂等 ----


def test_request_id_is_idempotent():
    match = new_match()
    first = match.current
    assert match.bid(first, 3, request_id="req-1").ok
    state_snapshot = (match.state, match.landlord, dict(match.bids))
    duplicate = match.bid(first, 3, request_id="req-1")
    assert duplicate.duplicate is True
    assert (match.state, match.landlord, dict(match.bids)) == state_snapshot


def test_request_id_is_scoped_per_player():
    """两个玩家用同一个 request_id 时，不能互相把对方的动作吞掉。"""
    match = new_match()
    first = match.current
    assert match.bid(first, 0, request_id="c4").ok
    second = match.current
    assert second != first
    action = match.bid(second, 2, request_id="c4")  # 同一个编号，但换了玩家
    assert action.ok and not action.duplicate
    assert match.bids[second] == 2, "第二个玩家的叫分必须真正生效"


# --------------------------------------------------------------- 视图 ----


def test_privacy_of_views():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    landlord = match.landlord
    view = match.view_for(landlord)
    assert "hand" in view and len(view["hand"]) == 20
    public = match.public_view()
    assert "hand" not in public
    assert "hands" not in public
    assert set(public["hand_counts"]) == set(match.players)


def test_spectator_view_has_no_hand():
    match = to_playing(new_match())
    view = match.view_for(None)
    assert "hand" not in view
    assert view["landlord"] == match.landlord
````

### `tests/test_m2.py`（391 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""M2：牌桌协议、状态裁决、观战、断线托管与重连恢复。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

import pytest

from bots.doudizhu_bot import DoudizhuBot
from client.net import Connection, ServerError
from client.session import ClientSession
from server.protocol import ErrCode

ROOM = "牌桌1"


async def make_seated(server, names, room=ROOM):
    """登录并坐上牌桌，返回 (连接, 机器人) 列表与角色信息。"""
    conns, roles = [], []
    for name in names:
        conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
        await conn.login(name)
        reply = await conn.join_room(room)
        conns.append(conn)
        roles.append(reply["data"].get("role"))
    return conns, roles


async def ready_all(conns):
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.request("room_ready", {})


async def test_seats_and_spectators(server):
    conns, roles = await make_seated(server, ["p0", "p1", "p2", "p3", "p4"])
    assert roles == ["player", "player", "player", "spectator", "spectator"]

    extra = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await extra.login("p5")
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await extra.join_room(ROOM)
    assert exc.value.code == ErrCode.ROOM_FULL
    for conn in conns + [extra]:
        await conn.close()


async def test_ready_deals_private_hands(server):
    conns, _ = await make_seated(server, ["d0", "d1", "d2"])
    await ready_all(conns)

    hands = []
    for conn in conns:
        frame = await conn.wait_for("game_start", timeout=3.0)
        assert len(frame["data"]["hand"]) == 17
        hands.append(set(frame["data"]["hand"]))

    # 三家手牌互不重叠，合计 51 张
    assert len(hands[0] | hands[1] | hands[2]) == 51
    # 公开视图不含任何手牌
    public = await conns[0].wait_for("table_state", timeout=3.0)
    assert "hand" not in public["data"]
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.close()


async def test_bid_three_makes_landlord_and_reveals_bottom(server):
    conns, _ = await make_seated(server, ["e0", "e1", "e2"])
    await ready_all(conns)
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.wait_for("game_start", timeout=3.0)

    state = await conns[0].wait_for("table_state", timeout=3.0, match={"state": "BIDDING"})
    first = state["data"]["current"]
    bidder = conns[["e0", "e1", "e2"].index(first)]
    await bidder.request("bid", {"score": 3})

    for conn in conns:
        landlord_event = await conn.wait_for("landlord", timeout=3.0)
        assert landlord_event["data"]["landlord"] == first
        assert len(landlord_event["data"]["bottom"]) == 3

    hand = await bidder.wait_for("hand", timeout=3.0)
    assert len(hand["data"]["hand"]) == 20, "地主应拿到三张底牌"
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.close()


async def test_play_rules_are_enforced(server):
    conns, _ = await make_seated(server, ["f0", "f1", "f2"])
    await ready_all(conns)
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.wait_for("game_start", timeout=3.0)

    state = await conns[0].wait_for("table_state", timeout=3.0, match={"state": "BIDDING"})
    first = state["data"]["current"]
    index = ["f0", "f1", "f2"].index(first)
    landlord_conn, landlord_bot = conns[index], None
    await landlord_conn.request("bid", {"score": 3})
    hand = await landlord_conn.wait_for("hand", timeout=3.0)
    cards = hand["data"]["hand"]

    # 不是自己回合
    other = conns[(index + 1) % 3]
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await other.request("play", {"cards": [cards[0]]})
    assert exc.value.code == "NOT_YOUR_TURN"

    # 先手不能过牌
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await landlord_conn.request("pass", {})
    assert exc.value.code == "CANNOT_PASS"

    # 手里没有的牌
    foreign = next(code for code in ("3s", "4h", "5d", "6c", "7s", "8h", "9d")
                   if code not in cards)
    with pytest.raises(ServerError) as exc:
        await landlord_conn.request("play", {"cards": [foreign]})
    assert exc.value.code == "NOT_IN_HAND"

    # 合法出牌
    ok = await landlord_conn.request("play", {"cards": [cards[-1]]})
    assert ok["data"]["action"] == "play"
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.close()


async def test_spectator_sees_only_public_state(server):
    conns, _ = await make_seated(server, ["g0", "g1", "g2"])
    await ready_all(conns)
    spectator = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await spectator.login("watcher")
    reply = await spectator.join_room(ROOM)
    assert reply["data"]["role"] == "spectator"

    state = await spectator.wait_for("table_state", timeout=3.0)
    assert "hand" not in state["data"]
    assert "hand_counts" in state["data"]
    for conn in conns + [spectator]:
        await conn.close()


async def test_three_bots_finish_a_game(server):
    """端到端验收：三个机器人通过协议打完整局，手牌不泄露，分数为 0 和。"""
    conns = [await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port) for _ in range(3)]
    bots = []
    for index, conn in enumerate(conns):
        await conn.login(f"bot{index}")
        bots.append(DoudizhuBot(f"bot{index}", conn, ROOM))
    for bot in bots:
        await bot.join_and_ready()

    results = await asyncio.wait_for(
        asyncio.gather(*(bot.play_until_end(60) for bot in bots)), 75
    )
    result = results[0]
    assert result["winner"] in [b.nickname for b in bots]
    assert sum(result["scores"].values()) == 0
    assert all(not bot.errors for bot in bots)
    # 每个机器人结束时都不知道别人的手牌（只知道张数）
    for bot in bots:
        assert len(bot.hand) <= 20
        assert set(bot.hand_counts) == {b.nickname for b in bots}


async def test_disconnected_player_is_auto_played(server_factory):
    server = await server_factory(auto_play_delay_ms=150, sweep_interval_ms=100)
    conns, _ = await make_seated(server, ["h0", "h1", "h2"])
    await ready_all(conns)
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.wait_for("game_start", timeout=3.0)

    state = await conns[0].wait_for("table_state", timeout=3.0, match={"state": "BIDDING"})
    first = state["data"]["current"]
    index = ["h0", "h1", "h2"].index(first)
    await conns[index].request("bid", {"score": 3})
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.wait_for("landlord", timeout=3.0)

    table = server.hub.rooms[ROOM].table
    before = len(table.match.history)
    await conns[index].close()  # 地主掉线

    # 服务端应在托管延迟后替他自动出牌，牌局继续
    for _ in range(40):
        if len(table.match.history) > before:
            break
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    assert len(table.match.history) > before, "掉线玩家应由服务端托管自动出牌"
    assert table.seats[first] is None, "掉线期间保留座位并标记离线"
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.close()


async def test_table_plays_a_second_game(server):
    """一局结束后，三家重新准备应能开新的一局。"""
    conns = [await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port) for _ in range(3)]
    bots = []
    for index, conn in enumerate(conns):
        await conn.login(f"again{index}")
        bots.append(DoudizhuBot(f"again{index}", conn, ROOM))
    for bot in bots:
        await bot.join_and_ready()

    await asyncio.wait_for(asyncio.gather(*(bot.play_until_end(30) for bot in bots)), 45)
    table = server.hub.rooms[ROOM].table
    assert table.match.state.value == "SETTLED"
    first_match = table.match

    for bot in bots:
        bot.finished = asyncio.Event()
        bot.result = None
        bot._action_key = None
        await bot.conn.request("room_ready", {})

    # 机器人打得很快，第二局可能瞬间就打完了；关键是"换了新的一局"且能正常结算
    results = await asyncio.wait_for(
        asyncio.gather(*(bot.play_until_end(30) for bot in bots)), 45
    )
    assert table.match is not first_match, "第二局应当是新的对局对象"
    assert sum(results[0]["scores"].values()) == 0


async def test_online_auto_play_takes_over_my_turn(server_factory):
    """在线托管：人还在线，但轮到他时由服务端自动出牌。"""
    server = await server_factory(auto_play_delay_ms=150, sweep_interval_ms=100)
    conns, _ = await make_seated(server, ["t0", "t1", "t2"])
    await ready_all(conns)
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.wait_for("game_start", timeout=3.0)

    state = await conns[0].wait_for("table_state", timeout=3.0, match={"state": "BIDDING"})
    first = state["data"]["current"]
    index = ["t0", "t1", "t2"].index(first)
    landlord_conn = conns[index]
    await landlord_conn.request("bid", {"score": 3})
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.wait_for("landlord", timeout=3.0)

    # 地主开启托管后不再自己出牌
    await landlord_conn.request("auto_play", {"enabled": True})
    table = server.hub.rooms[ROOM].table
    before = len(table.match.history)
    for _ in range(40):
        if len(table.match.history) > before:
            break
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    assert len(table.match.history) > before, "托管后服务端应自动替他行动"
    assert first in table.auto_players
    assert table.seats[first] is not None, "在线托管不掉座（座位仍绑定着在线会话）"

    # 取回控制后不再自动打
    await landlord_conn.request("auto_play", {"enabled": False})
    assert first not in table.auto_players
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.close()


async def test_human_turn_has_no_time_limit(server_factory):
    """真人控制时不限时：放着不动也不会被服务端代打。"""
    server = await server_factory(auto_play_delay_ms=200, sweep_interval_ms=100)
    conns, _ = await make_seated(server, ["h0", "h1", "h2"])
    await ready_all(conns)
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.wait_for("game_start", timeout=3.0)

    state = await conns[0].wait_for("table_state", timeout=3.0, match={"state": "BIDDING"})
    first = state["data"]["current"]
    index = ["h0", "h1", "h2"].index(first)
    await conns[index].request("bid", {"score": 3})
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.wait_for("landlord", timeout=3.0)

    table = server.hub.rooms[ROOM].table
    before = len(table.match.history)
    await asyncio.sleep(1.0)   # 超过 auto_play_delay_ms，但地主由真人控制
    assert len(table.match.history) == before, "真人控制时不该被自动代打"
    assert table.public_state().get("deadline_in_ms") is None, "真人出牌不限时"
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.close()


async def test_log_line_comes_with_its_own_event():
    """每条事件自带它那一行日志，避免界面取“最近一行”造成重复与漏行。"""
    session = ClientSession()
    lines: list[str] = []
    session.on_event = lambda kind, data: lines.append(data["_line"]) if data.get("_line") else None

    await session._handle("bid", {"player": "a", "score": 1})
    await session._handle("bid", {"player": "b", "score": 0})
    await session._handle("play", {"player": "a", "cards_text": "3", "hand_count": 16})
    await session._handle("pass", {"player": "b"})

    assert lines == [
        "[叫分] a 叫 1 分",
        "[叫分] b 不叫",
        "[出牌] a: 3",
        "[过牌] b",
    ], "同一批事件必须各自带自己的那行日志"


async def test_log_shows_remaining_only_when_close_to_finish():
    """出牌记录平时不带剩余张数，只在剩 ≤3 张时提示。"""
    session = ClientSession()
    lines: list[str] = []
    session.on_event = lambda kind, data: lines.append(data["_line"]) if data.get("_line") else None

    await session._handle("play", {"player": "a", "cards_text": "3", "hand_count": 10})
    await session._handle("play", {"player": "a", "cards_text": "4", "hand_count": 3})
    await session._handle("play", {"player": "b", "cards_text": "5", "hand_count": 1})

    assert lines == ["[出牌] a: 3", "[出牌] a: 4　剩 3 张", "[出牌] b: 5　剩 1 张"]


async def test_chat_works_inside_a_table(server):
    """牌局内也能聊天：房间广播与私信都要能收到，并写进牌桌记录。"""
    session = ClientSession()
    await session.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await session.login("talker")
    await session.join_room(ROOM)

    other = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await other.login("mate")
    await other.join_room(ROOM)

    await session.say("大家好")
    await asyncio.sleep(0.3)
    assert "我: 大家好" in session.log, "自己发的聊天应显示为“我: …”"

    await other.request("room_msg", {"text": "收到"})
    await asyncio.sleep(0.3)
    assert "mate: 收到" in session.log, "牌桌上应能收到别人的聊天"

    await session.whisper("mate", "只跟你说")
    await asyncio.sleep(0.3)
    assert "（私信）我 → mate: 只跟你说" in session.log

    # 对方也能收到，并且在对方视角里标注"→ 我"
    other_log = ClientSession()
    await other_log.connect(server.host, server.port)
    await other_log.login("mate2")
    await other_log.join_room(ROOM)
    await session.whisper("mate2", "给你一条")
    await asyncio.sleep(0.3)
    assert "（私信）talker → 我: 给你一条" in other_log.log
    await other_log.close()
    await session.close()
    await other.close()


def test_jokers_are_displayed_in_chinese():
    """牌局记录里大小王显示为中文，协议编码仍是 BJ/CJ。"""
    from game.text import card_code, card_text, cards_text, parse_card

    big = parse_card("CJ")
    small = parse_card("BJ")
    assert card_text(small) == "小王"
    assert card_text(big) == "大王"
    assert cards_text([small, big]) == "小王 大王"
    assert card_code(small) == "BJ" and card_code(big) == "CJ"


async def test_resume_into_table_restores_hand(server):
    """断线后用 token 回到牌桌：昵称、房间、手牌都要恢复。"""
    conns, tokens = [], []
    for name in ("k0", "k1", "k2"):
        conn = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
        welcome = await conn.login(name)
        tokens.append(welcome["data"]["token"])
        await conn.join_room(ROOM)
        conns.append(conn)
    await ready_all(conns)
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.wait_for("game_start", timeout=3.0)

    await conns[0].close()  # 掉线，进入重连保留期
    for _ in range(40):
        if server.hub.reserved_count() == 1:
            break
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    assert server.hub.reserved_count() == 1

    again = await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port)
    resumed = await again.resume(tokens[0])
    assert resumed["data"]["nickname"] == "k0"
    assert resumed["data"]["room"] == ROOM, "重连应回到原牌桌"
    assert resumed["data"]["token"] != tokens[0], "重连应换发新 token"

    snapshot = await again.wait_for("game_state", timeout=3.0)
    assert len(snapshot["data"]["hand"]) == 17, "重连后应拿回自己的手牌"
    for conn in conns[1:] + [again]:
        await conn.close()
````

## 七、P0-5：10 客户端并发、机器人压测与慢客户端对照

**对应要求**

- 至少 10 个机器人客户端同时运行。
- 统计广播到达率、延迟和错误。
- 注入慢读、只连不读客户端，验证其他客户端仍可聊天。
- 日志能够定位具体连接。

**功能与代码的对应关系**

- `tests/test_acceptance_m0.py`：10 客户端同房间并发测试。
- `bots/run_bots.py`：机器人登录、发言、统计和慢客户端注入。
- `bots/run_table.py`：走网络完成一局斗地主。
- `bots/fill_seats.py`：用 AI 占住牌桌座位。
- `bots/doudizhu_bot.py`：机器人对局决策。

**实测结果**

- M0：10 客户端 × 20 轮，1800/1800 到达，p50 1.3 ms，p95 1.8 ms。
- M1：无慢客户端 3600/3600，p95 6.6 ms；注入一个只连不读客户端后
  3600/3600，p95 6.8 ms，慢客户端被单独断开。

结果文件见 `evidence/M0验证.md` 和 `evidence/M1验证.md`。

### 本功能完整代码

### `tests/test_acceptance_m0.py`（81 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""M0 验收：10 个客户端同时在线，房间内互相发消息全部到达。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import statistics
import time

from client.net import Connection


async def test_ten_clients_chat_in_one_room(server):
    client_count = 10
    rounds = 5
    room = "聊天房"

    conns = [await Connection.connect(server.host, server.port) for _ in range(client_count)]
    welcomes = []
    for index, conn in enumerate(conns):
        welcomes.append(await conn.login(f"bot{index}"))
    assert welcomes[-1]["data"]["online"] == client_count, "服务端应看到 10 个在线用户"
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.join_room(room)

    received: dict[int, set[str]] = {i: set() for i in range(client_count)}
    latencies: list[float] = []
    sent_at: dict[str, float] = {}
    stop = asyncio.Event()

    async def collect(index: int) -> None:
        conn = conns[index]
        while not stop.is_set():
            try:
                frame = await conn.recv(0.05)
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                continue
            except Exception:
                return
            if frame.get("type") != "room_msg":
                continue
            data = frame["data"]
            marker = data["text"].split()[-1]
            if marker.startswith(f"m-{index}-"):
                continue
            if marker in received[index]:
                continue
            received[index].add(marker)
            start = sent_at.get(marker)
            if start is not None:
                latencies.append((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000)

    collectors = [asyncio.create_task(collect(i)) for i in range(client_count)]

    for round_index in range(rounds):
        for index, conn in enumerate(conns):
            marker = f"m-{index}-{round_index}"
            sent_at[marker] = time.perf_counter()
            await conn.send("room_msg", {"text": f"并发测试 {marker}"})
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    await asyncio.sleep(1.5)
    stop.set()

    expected = {f"m-{i}-{r}" for i in range(client_count) for r in range(rounds)}
    for index in range(client_count):
        others = {m for m in expected if not m.startswith(f"m-{index}-")}
        missing = others - received[index]
        assert not missing, f"客户端 {index} 漏收 {len(missing)} 条: {sorted(missing)[:5]}"

    total_expected = rounds * client_count * (client_count - 1)
    assert len(latencies) == total_expected
    ordered = sorted(latencies)
    p50 = statistics.median(ordered)
    p95 = ordered[int(round(0.95 * (len(ordered) - 1)))]
    print(f"\n10 客户端 / {rounds} 轮：到达 {len(latencies)}/{total_expected}"
          f"，p50 {p50:.1f} ms，p95 {p95:.1f} ms")

    for task in collectors:
        task.cancel()
    await asyncio.gather(*collectors, return_exceptions=True)
    for conn in conns:
        await conn.close()
````

### `bots/run_bots.py`（190 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""M0 机器人压测：N 个客户端同时在线，互相发消息并统计到达率与延迟。

用法（项目根目录）：
    .venv\\Scripts\\python.exe bots\\run_bots.py --clients 10 --rounds 5
    .venv\\Scripts\\python.exe bots\\run_bots.py --host 127.0.0.1 --port 9011
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import random
import statistics
import socket
import sys
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

try:
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
except Exception:
    pass

from client.net import Connection  # noqa: E402
from server.framing import encode_frame  # noqa: E402


def spawn_slow_client(host: str, port: int, nickname: str, room: str) -> threading.Thread:
    """慢客户端：连上、登录、进房间，然后**永不读取**，用于对照实验。"""

    def worker() -> None:
        try:
            sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_RCVBUF, 2048)
            sock.connect((host, port))
            sock.sendall(encode_frame({"type": "login", "data": {"nickname": nickname},
                                       "request_id": "slow-1"}))
            time.sleep(0.3)
            sock.sendall(encode_frame({"type": "join_room", "data": {"room": room},
                                       "request_id": "slow-2"}))
            time.sleep(3600)
        except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover
            print(f"[慢客户端] 异常: {exc}")

    thread = threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    return thread


class Bot:
    """压测用机器人：登录、进房、发消息并统计到达率与延迟。"""
    def __init__(self, nick: str, args, stats: dict) -> None:
        """初始化机器人与去重集合。"""
        self.nick = nick
        self.args = args
        self.stats = stats
        self.conn: Connection | None = None
        self.sent: dict[str, float] = {}
        self.task: asyncio.Task | None = None
        self.seen: set[str] = set()  # 每个机器人各自去重，不能共用

    async def start(self) -> bool:
        """连接、登录、进房并启动收消息任务。"""
        try:
            self.conn = await Connection.connect(self.args.host, self.args.port)
            await self.conn.login(self.nick)
            await self.conn.join_room(self.args.room)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[{self.nick}] 连接/登录失败: {exc}")
            return False
        self.stats["online"] += 1
        self.task = asyncio.create_task(self._reader())
        return True

    async def _reader(self) -> None:
        """统计收到的广播消息与延迟。"""
        assert self.conn is not None
        while True:
            try:
                frame = await self.conn.recv()
            except Exception:
                return
            if frame.get("type") != "room_msg":
                continue
            data = frame["data"]
            sender = data.get("from")
            if sender == self.nick:
                continue
            marker = _marker_of(data.get("text", ""))
            if marker is None or marker in self.seen:
                continue
            self.seen.add(marker)
            self.stats["received"] += 1
            sent_at = self.stats["sent_at"].get(marker)
            if sent_at is not None:
                self.stats["latencies"].append((time.perf_counter() - sent_at) * 1000)

    async def say(self, index: int, run_id: str) -> None:
        """发一条带唯一标记的压测消息。"""
        assert self.conn is not None
        marker = f"m-{self.nick}-{index}-{run_id}"
        self.stats["sent_at"][marker] = time.perf_counter()
        self.stats["sent"] += 1
        try:
            await self.conn.room_msg(f"{marker} " + "x" * self.args.payload)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[{self.nick}] 发送失败: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc!r}")
            self.stats["errors"] = self.stats.get("errors", 0) + 1

    async def stop(self) -> None:
        """主动登出并关闭连接。"""
        if self.task:
            self.task.cancel()
        if self.conn:
            # 主动退出：服务端会立即释放昵称，不会进入重连保留期
            try:
                await asyncio.wait_for(self.conn.send("logout"), 1.0)
                await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
            except Exception:
                pass
            await self.conn.close()


def _marker_of(text: str) -> str | None:
    """从消息文本里取出压测标记。"""
    for token in text.split():
        if token.startswith("m-"):
            return token
    return None


async def main() -> int:
    """多客户端压测入口。"""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=9011)
    parser.add_argument("--clients", type=int, default=10)
    parser.add_argument("--rounds", type=int, default=5)
    parser.add_argument("--room", default="聊天房")
    parser.add_argument("--interval", type=float, default=0.05)
    parser.add_argument("--drain", type=float, default=2.0)
    parser.add_argument("--prefix", default="bot")
    parser.add_argument("--payload", type=int, default=0, help="每条消息追加的填充字符数")
    parser.add_argument("--slow", type=int, default=0, help="注入几个只连不读的慢客户端")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    run_id = "".join(random.choice("abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789") for _ in range(4))
    stats = {"online": 0, "sent": 0, "received": 0,
             "sent_at": {}, "latencies": [], "errors": 0}

    bots = [Bot(f"{args.prefix}{i}", args, stats) for i in range(args.clients)]
    results = await asyncio.gather(*(bot.start() for bot in bots))
    online = sum(1 for ok in results if ok)
    print(f"同时在线: {online}/{args.clients}")
    if online < args.clients:
        print("提示：有客户端没连上，后面的到达率会偏低")

    for index in range(args.slow):
        # 带 run 后缀：异常断开的昵称会保留 60 秒，重复用同名会导致登录被拒
        spawn_slow_client(args.host, args.port, f"slow{index}-{run_id}", args.room)
    if args.slow:
        print(f"已注入 {args.slow} 个慢客户端（只连不读）")
        await asyncio.sleep(0.5)

    for index in range(args.rounds):
        await asyncio.gather(*(bot.say(index, run_id) for bot in bots if bot.conn))
        await asyncio.sleep(args.interval)
    await asyncio.sleep(args.drain)

    expected = args.rounds * online * max(online - 1, 0)
    rate = (stats["received"] / expected * 100) if expected else 0.0
    print(f"广播到达: {stats['received']}/{expected}（{rate:.1f}%）")
    if stats["errors"]:
        print(f"发送错误: {stats['errors']} 次")
    if stats["latencies"]:
        ordered = sorted(stats["latencies"])
        p50 = statistics.median(ordered)
        p95 = ordered[min(len(ordered) - 1, int(round(0.95 * (len(ordered) - 1))))]
        print(f"广播延迟: p50 {p50:.1f} ms  p95 {p95:.1f} ms  最大 {max(ordered):.1f} ms")
    print(f"结论: {'通过' if online == args.clients and rate >= 99.0 else '未通过'}")

    await asyncio.gather(*(bot.stop() for bot in bots))
    return 0 if online == args.clients and rate >= 99.0 else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(asyncio.run(main()))
````

### `bots/run_table.py`（95 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""三个机器人打完整局斗地主，逐手打印，并检查手牌隐私。

用法（先启动服务端）：
    .venv\\Scripts\\python.exe bots\\run_table.py --room 牌桌1
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import random
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

try:
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
except Exception:
    pass

from bots.doudizhu_bot import DoudizhuBot  # noqa: E402
from client.net import Connection  # noqa: E402


async def main() -> int:
    """三机器人走网络打完整局的演示入口。"""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=9011)
    parser.add_argument("--room", default="牌桌1")
    parser.add_argument("--spectators", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--password", default="123456")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    # 严格模式（默认）下只能用已存在的账号，这里取预置账号的后三个
    names = ["wanjia3", "wanjia4", "wanjia5"]
    password = args.password

    conns = [await Connection.connect(args.host, args.port) for _ in range(3)]
    for conn, name in zip(conns, names):
        await conn.login(name, password)
    bots = [DoudizhuBot(name, conn, args.room) for name, conn in zip(names, conns)]

    spectators = []
    for index in range(args.spectators):
        conn = await Connection.connect(args.host, args.port)
        await conn.login(f"wanjia{index + 1}", password)
        spectators.append(conn)

    try:
        for bot in bots:
            await bot.join_and_ready()
        for conn in spectators:
            await conn.request("spectate", {"room": args.room})
        print(f"牌桌 {args.room}: {', '.join(names)} 全部准备，观战 {len(spectators)} 人")

        results = await asyncio.gather(*(bot.play_until_end(timeout=180) for bot in bots))

        print("\n== 对局过程 ==")
        for line in bots[0].log:
            print("  " + line)

        result = results[0]
        print("\n== 结算 ==")
        print(f"  赢家: {result.get('winner')}（地主 {result.get('landlord')}）")
        print(f"  地主{'获胜' if result.get('landlord_win') else '落败'}，"
              f"春天={result.get('spring')}，炸弹={result.get('bombs')}，倍数 ×{result.get('multiplier')}")
        for player, score in (result.get("scores") or {}).items():
            print(f"  {player}: {'+' if score > 0 else ''}{score}")
        print(f"  分数合计: {sum((result.get('scores') or {}).values())}")

        # 手牌隐私：任何机器人都不该收到别人的手牌
        leaked = [b.nickname for b in bots if b.hand and len(b.hand) > 20]
        print(f"\n== 隐私检查 ==")
        print(f"  收到超过 20 张手牌的机器人: {leaked or '无'}")
        print(f"  三个机器人结束时手牌张数: {[len(b.hand) for b in bots]}")

        ok = not leaked and all(not b.errors for b in bots)
        print(f"  结论: {'通过' if ok else '未通过'}")
        return 0 if ok else 1
    finally:
        for bot in bots:
            await bot.stop()
        for conn in conns + spectators:
            try:
                await conn.send("logout")
                await asyncio.sleep(0.02)
            except Exception:
                pass
            await conn.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(asyncio.run(main()))
````

### `bots/fill_seats.py`（100 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""AI 陪打：占住牌桌的几个座位，自动出牌，直到你停止脚本。

用法（先启动服务端，再开你的窗口客户端进同一张牌桌）：

    .venv\\Scripts\\python.exe bots\\fill_seats.py --room 牌桌2 --ai 2

它会打印每个 AI 的行动，方便你对照牌局。按 Ctrl+C 结束。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import random
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

try:
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
except Exception:
    pass

from bots.doudizhu_bot import DoudizhuBot  # noqa: E402
from client.net import Connection  # noqa: E402


async def printer(bots: list[DoudizhuBot], stop: asyncio.Event) -> None:
    """把每个 AI 的日志增量打印出来。"""
    printed = {bot.nickname: 0 for bot in bots}
    while not stop.is_set():
        for bot in bots:
            lines = bot.log[printed[bot.nickname]:]
            for line in lines:
                print(f"  [{bot.nickname}] {line}")
            printed[bot.nickname] = len(bot.log)
            if bot.result:
                result = bot.result
                print(f"  === 本局结束：赢家 {result.get('winner')}，"
                      f"倍数 ×{result.get('multiplier')}，分数 {result.get('scores')} ===")
                bot.result = None
        await asyncio.sleep(0.3)


async def main() -> int:
    """AI 陪打入口：占住座位并一直自动出牌。"""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=9011)
    parser.add_argument("--room", default="牌桌2")
    parser.add_argument("--ai", type=int, default=2, help="占几个座位（最多 3；留给你自己的那 1 个不用填）")
    parser.add_argument("--accounts", default="wanjia4,wanjia5",
                        help="AI 使用哪些账号（默认取预置账号 wanjia4/wanjia5；严格模式下必须是已存在的账号）")
    parser.add_argument("--password", default="123456")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    accounts = [a.strip() for a in args.accounts.split(",") if a.strip()]
    bots: list[DoudizhuBot] = []
    conns: list[Connection] = []
    tasks: list[asyncio.Task] = []
    stop = asyncio.Event()

    for index in range(args.ai):
        conn = await Connection.connect(args.host, args.port)
        nickname = accounts[index % len(accounts)] if accounts else f"ai{index + 1}"
        await conn.login(nickname, args.password)
        bot = DoudizhuBot(nickname, conn, args.room)
        conns.append(conn)
        bots.append(bot)
        tasks.append(asyncio.create_task(bot.run_forever()))
        print(f"AI 就位：{nickname} → {args.room}")
        await asyncio.sleep(0.4)

    print(f"\n现在用窗口客户端进 {args.room} 坐下并点“准备”，凑满 {3} 人就会开局。")
    print("（AI 会自动叫分与出牌；你想接手某一手时，可以在界面里点“托管/取回控制”）\n")

    printing = asyncio.create_task(printer(bots, stop))
    try:
        await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
    except asyncio.CancelledError:
        pass
    finally:
        stop.set()
        printing.cancel()
        for conn in conns:
            try:
                await conn.send("logout")
                await asyncio.sleep(0.02)
            except Exception:
                pass
            await conn.close()
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        raise SystemExit(asyncio.run(main()))
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n已停止 AI 陪打")
````

### `bots/doudizhu_bot.py`（195 行，以下为完整代码）

````python
"""会打牌的机器人：通过协议连接牌桌，用 game.ai 决策，直到一局结束。

它同时承担两个用途：

* `scripts`/`bots` 下的演示与压测（三个机器人自动打完整局）；
* 自动化测试里的端到端客户​​端（验证"网络层 + 规则引擎"整条链路）。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import traceback
from typing import Any

from game import ai
from game.text import card_code, cards_text, parse_card
from client.net import Connection


class DoudizhuBot:
    """走协议的斗地主机器人：维护自己的手牌与公开状态，用 game.ai 决策。"""
    def __init__(self, nickname: str, conn: Connection, room: str) -> None:
        """初始化机器人状态与收消息任务。"""
        self.nickname = nickname
        self.conn = conn
        self.room = room
        self.hand: list[int] = []
        self.state: str | None = None
        self.current: str | None = None
        self.landlord: str | None = None
        self.table: list[int] = []
        self.bids: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.hand_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.multiplier = 1
        self.log: list[str] = []
        self.result: dict | None = None
        self.finished = asyncio.Event()
        self._action_key: tuple | None = None
        self.history_len = 0
        self._task: asyncio.Task | None = None
        self.errors: list[str] = []

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 生命周期 ----

    async def join_and_ready(self) -> None:
        """进房并准备，同时启动收消息循环。"""
        await self.conn.join_room(self.room)
        self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._loop())
        await self.conn.request("room_ready", {})

    async def play_until_end(self, timeout: float = 120.0) -> dict:
        """等待一局结束并返回结算数据。"""
        await asyncio.wait_for(self.finished.wait(), timeout)
        assert self.result is not None
        return self.result

    async def run_forever(self, rounds: int | None = None) -> None:
        """一直坐在牌桌上：每局结束自动再准备，供"AI 陪打"使用。"""
        await self.join_and_ready()
        played = 0
        while True:
            await self.finished.wait()
            played += 1
            if rounds is not None and played >= rounds:
                return
            self.finished = asyncio.Event()
            self.result = None
            self._action_key = None
            await asyncio.sleep(0.3)
            await self.conn.request("room_ready", {})

    async def stop(self) -> None:
        """停止收消息任务。"""
        if self._task:
            self._task.cancel()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 收消息 ----

    async def _loop(self) -> None:
        """收消息主循环。"""
        try:
            while True:
                frame = await self.conn.recv()
                await self._handle(frame)
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - 演示脚本里直接打印
            self.errors.append(f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
````
